===== README.md =====
# 股票記帳本雲端版

這是股票記帳本 V1.0.1 的 Streamlit Cloud + Supabase 同步版本。

部署時需要在 Streamlit Cloud 的 Secrets 裡加入：

```toml
[app]
password = "請換成你自己的登入密碼"

[supabase]
url = "你的 Supabase Project URL"
anon_key = "你的 Supabase anon key"
table = "stock_ledger_state"
row_id = "main"
```

===== app.js =====

els.form.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();

  const holding = {
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    symbol: normalizeSymbol(els.symbol.value),
    name: els.name.value.trim(),
    shares: toNumber(els.shares.value),
    avgCost: toNumber(els.cost.value),
    currentPrice: toOptionalNumber(els.price.value),
    purchaseDate: todayISO(),
    lastUpdated: "",
  };

  state.holdings.push(holding);
  saveAndRender("已新增台股。");
  els.form.reset();
  els.symbol.focus();
});

els.usForm.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();
  state.usHoldings.push({
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    symbol: normalizeSymbol(els.usSymbol.value),
    shares: toNumber(els.usShares.value),
    avgCost: toNumber(els.usCost.value),
    currentPrice: toOptionalNumber(els.usPrice.value),
    purchaseDate: todayISO(),
    lastUpdated: "",
  });
  saveAndRender("已新增美股。");
  els.usForm.reset();
  els.usSymbol.focus();
});

els.fundForm.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();

  state.funds.push({
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    name: els.fundName.value.trim(),
    cost: toNumber(els.fundCost.value),
    currentValue: toNumber(els.fundAmount.value),
    lastUpdated: "",
  });
  saveAndRender("已新增基金。");
  els.fundForm.reset();
  els.fundName.focus();
});

els.cryptoForm.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();
  state.cryptoContracts.push({
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    name: normalizeSymbol(els.cryptoName.value),
    side: els.cryptoSide.value === "short" ? "short" : "long",
    leverage: Math.max(1, toNumber(els.cryptoLeverage.value)),
    marginUsdt: toNumber(els.cryptoMargin.value),
    quantity: toNumber(els.cryptoQuantity.value),
    entryPrice: toNumber(els.cryptoEntryPrice.value),
    currentPrice: toNumber(els.cryptoCurrentPrice.value),
  });
  saveAndRender("已新增加密貨幣合約。");
  els.cryptoForm.reset();
  els.cryptoName.focus();
});

els.cryptoList.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
  const input = event.target.closest("[data-crypto-field]");
  if (!input) return;
  const contract = findCryptoContract(input.dataset.cryptoId);
  if (!contract) return;
  const field = input.dataset.cryptoField;
  contract[field] = field === "side"
    ? (input.value === "short" ? "short" : "long")
    : field === "leverage"
      ? Math.max(1, toNumber(input.value))
      : toNumber(input.value);
  saveAndRender("已更新加密貨幣合約。");
});

els.cryptoList.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
  const button = event.target.closest("[data-delete-crypto-id]");
  if (!button) return;
  const contract = findCryptoContract(button.dataset.deleteCryptoId);
  if (!contract || !confirm(`確定刪除 ${contract.name || "這筆加密貨幣合約"}？`)) return;
  openCryptoCards.delete(contract.id);
  state.cryptoContracts = state.cryptoContracts.filter((item) => item.id !== contract.id);
  saveAndRender("已刪除加密貨幣合約。");
});

els.holdingsBody.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
  const input = event.target.closest("[data-price-id]");
  if (!input) return;
  const holding = findHolding(input.dataset.priceId);
  if (!holding) return;
  holding.currentPrice = toOptionalNumber(input.value);
  holding.lastUpdated = holding.currentPrice === null ? "" : "手動";
  saveAndRender();
});

els.holdingsBody.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
  const editBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-id]");
  if (editBtn) {
    editingHoldingId = editBtn.dataset.editId;
    renderHoldings();
    return;
  }

  const cancelBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-cancel-id]");
  if (cancelBtn) {
    editingHoldingId = "";
    renderHoldings();
    return;
  }

  const saveBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-save-id]");
  if (saveBtn) {
    const holding = findHolding(saveBtn.dataset.editSaveId);
    const editRow = saveBtn.closest("[data-edit-row]");
    if (!holding || !editRow) return;
    const symbol = normalizeSymbol(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-field='symbol']").value);
    if (!symbol) {
      setStatus("股號不能空白。");
      return;
    }
    holding.name = editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-field='name']").value.trim();
    holding.symbol = symbol;
    holding.shares = toNumber(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-field='shares']").value);
    holding.avgCost = toNumber(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-field='avgCost']").value);
    holding.purchaseDate = parseDateInput(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-field='purchaseDate']").value) || holding.purchaseDate;
    editingHoldingId = "";
    saveAndRender("已更新股票資料。");
    return;
  }

  const deleteBtn = event.target.closest("[data-delete-id]");
  if (!deleteBtn) return;
  const holding = findHolding(deleteBtn.dataset.deleteId);
  if (!holding) return;
  openTradeDecision("tw", holding);
});

els.usHoldingsBody.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
  const priceInput = event.target.closest("[data-us-price-id]");
  if (!priceInput) return;
  const holding = findUsHolding(priceInput.dataset.usPriceId);
  if (!holding) return;
  holding.currentPrice = toOptionalNumber(priceInput.value);
  holding.lastUpdated = "手動";
  saveAndRender("已更新美股資料。");
});

els.usHoldingsBody.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
  const editBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-us-id]");
  if (editBtn) {
    editingUsHoldingId = editBtn.dataset.editUsId;
    renderUsHoldings();
    return;
  }
  const cancelBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-us-cancel-id]");
  if (cancelBtn) {
    editingUsHoldingId = "";
    renderUsHoldings();
    return;
  }
  const saveBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-us-save-id]");
  if (saveBtn) {
    const holding = findUsHolding(saveBtn.dataset.editUsSaveId);
    const editRow = saveBtn.closest("[data-edit-us-row]");
    if (!holding || !editRow) return;
    holding.symbol = normalizeSymbol(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-us-field='symbol']").value);
    holding.shares = toNumber(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-us-field='shares']").value);
    holding.avgCost = toNumber(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-us-field='avgCost']").value);
    holding.purchaseDate = parseDateInput(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-us-field='purchaseDate']").value) || holding.purchaseDate;
    editingUsHoldingId = "";
    saveAndRender("已更新美股資料。");
    return;
  }
  const deleteBtn = event.target.closest("[data-delete-us-id]");
  if (!deleteBtn) return;
  const holding = findUsHolding(deleteBtn.dataset.deleteUsId);
  if (!holding) return;
  openTradeDecision("us", holding);
});

els.fundsBody.addEventListener("change", (event) => {
  const valueInput = event.target.closest("[data-fund-value-id]");
  const costInput = event.target.closest("[data-fund-cost-id]");
  if (!valueInput && !costInput) return;
  const fund = findFund(valueInput?.dataset.fundValueId || costInput?.dataset.fundCostId);
  if (!fund) return;
  if (valueInput) fund.currentValue = toOptionalNumber(valueInput.value) ?? 0;
  if (costInput) fund.cost = toOptionalNumber(costInput.value) ?? 0;
  fund.lastUpdated = "手動";
  saveAndRender();
});

els.cash.addEventListener("change", () => {
  state.cash = toNumber(els.cash.value);
  els.cash.value = state.cash ? String(state.cash) : "";
  saveAndRender("已更新現金水位。");
});

els.usdTwdRate.addEventListener("change", () => {
  state.usdTwdRate = toNumber(els.usdTwdRate.value);
  els.usdTwdRate.value = state.usdTwdRate ? String(state.usdTwdRate) : "";
  saveAndRender("已更新共用台幣匯率。");
});

els.usCashUsd.addEventListener("change", () => {
  state.usCashUsd = toNumber(els.usCashUsd.value);
  els.usCashUsd.value = state.usCashUsd ? String(state.usCashUsd) : "";
  saveAndRender("已更新美股美元現金。");
});

els.fundsBody.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
  const editBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-fund-id]");
  if (editBtn) {
    editingFundId = editBtn.dataset.editFundId;
    renderFunds();
    return;
  }

  const cancelBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-fund-cancel-id]");
  if (cancelBtn) {
    editingFundId = "";
    renderFunds();
    return;
  }

  const saveBtn = event.target.closest("[data-edit-fund-save-id]");
  if (saveBtn) {
    const fund = findFund(saveBtn.dataset.editFundSaveId);
    const editRow = saveBtn.closest("[data-edit-fund-row]");
    if (!fund || !editRow) return;
    const name = editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-fund-field='name']").value.trim();
    if (!name) {
      setStatus("基金名稱不能空白。");
      return;
    }
    fund.name = name;
    fund.cost = toNumber(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-fund-field='cost']").value);
    fund.currentValue = toNumber(editRow.querySelector("[data-edit-fund-field='currentValue']").value);
    fund.lastUpdated = "手動";
    editingFundId = "";
    saveAndRender("已更新基金資料。");
    return;
  }

  const deleteBtn = event.target.closest("[data-delete-fund-id]");
  if (!deleteBtn) return;
  const fund = findFund(deleteBtn.dataset.deleteFundId);
  if (!fund) return;
  if (!confirm(`確定刪除 ${fund.name || "這筆基金"}？`)) return;
  const index = state.funds.findIndex((item) => item.id === deleteBtn.dataset.deleteFundId);
  if (index >= 0) {
    state.funds.splice(index, 1);
    if (editingFundId === deleteBtn.dataset.deleteFundId) editingFundId = "";
    saveAndRender("已刪除基金。");
  }
});

els.settleBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
  state.cash = toNumber(els.cash.value);
  state.usdTwdRate = toNumber(els.usdTwdRate.value);
  state.usCashUsd = toNumber(els.usCashUsd.value);
  if (!state.holdings.length && !state.usHoldings.length && !state.funds.length && !state.cryptoContracts.length && !state.cash && !state.usCashUsd) {
    setStatus("請先新增台股、美股、基金、加密貨幣合約或現金水位。");
    return;
  }

  const date = parseDateInput(els.settlementDate.value) || todayISO();
  els.settlementDate.value = formatDateForInput(date);
  let stockTotal = 0;
  let fundTotal = 0;
  let stockCost = 0;
  let usStockTotal = 0;
  let usStockMarketValue = 0;
  let usStockCost = 0;
  let fundCost = 0;
  let cryptoTotal = 0;
  let cryptoCost = 0;
  const prices = {};
  const usPrices = {};
  const fundValues = {};
  const cryptoValues = {};

  for (const input of els.cryptoList.querySelectorAll("[data-crypto-field]")) {
    const contract = findCryptoContract(input.dataset.cryptoId);
    if (!contract) continue;
    const field = input.dataset.cryptoField;
    contract[field] = field === "side"
      ? (input.value === "short" ? "short" : "long")
      : field === "leverage"
        ? Math.max(1, toNumber(input.value))
        : toNumber(input.value);
  }

  for (const input of els.usHoldingsBody.querySelectorAll("[data-us-price-id]")) {
    const holding = findUsHolding(input.dataset.usPriceId);
    if (!holding) continue;
    holding.currentPrice = toOptionalNumber(input.value);
  }

  for (const holding of state.holdings) {
    const price = holding.currentPrice ?? holding.avgCost;
    prices[holding.id] = price;
    holding.currentPrice = price;
    stockTotal += holding.shares * price;
    stockCost += holding.shares * holding.avgCost;
  }

  for (const holding of state.usHoldings) {
    const price = holding.currentPrice ?? holding.avgCost;
    const fxRate = state.usdTwdRate || 0;
    usPrices[holding.id] = { price, fxRate };
    holding.currentPrice = price;
    usStockMarketValue += holding.shares * price * fxRate;
    usStockCost += holding.shares * holding.avgCost * fxRate;
  }

  const usCashUsd = toNumber(state.usCashUsd);
  const usCashTwd = usCashUsd * (state.usdTwdRate || 0);
  usStockTotal = usStockMarketValue + usCashTwd;

  for (const fund of state.funds) {
    const value = fund.currentValue ?? 0;
    fundValues[fund.id] = value;
    fund.currentValue = value;
    fundTotal += value;
    fundCost += fund.cost || 0;
  }

  for (const contract of state.cryptoContracts) {
    const position = calculateCryptoPosition(contract);
    cryptoValues[contract.id] = position.currentValueTwd;
    cryptoTotal += position.currentValueTwd;
    cryptoCost += position.costTwd;
  }

  state.currentFundTotal = fundTotal;
  const existingIndex = state.history.findIndex((item) => item.date === date);
  const existingRecord = existingIndex >= 0 ? state.history[existingIndex] : null;
  const note = els.settlementNote.value.trim() || existingRecord?.note || "";
  const cash = toNumber(state.cash);
  const total = stockTotal + usStockTotal + fundTotal + cryptoTotal + cash;
  const cost = stockCost + usStockCost + fundCost + cryptoCost;
  const record = {
    date,
    total,
    stockTotal,
    usStockTotal,
    usStockMarketValue,
    usCashUsd,
    usCashTwd,
    fundTotal,
    cryptoTotal,
    cash,
    cashRecorded: cash > 0,
    stockCost,
    usStockCost,
    fundCost,
    cryptoCost,
    cost,
    pnl: stockTotal + usStockMarketValue + fundTotal + cryptoTotal - cost,
    prices,
    usPrices,
    fundValues,
    cryptoValues,
    note,
  };
  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    state.history[existingIndex] = record;
  } else {
    state.history.push(record);
  }
  state.history.sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
  els.settlementNote.value = "";

  saveAndRender(`已記錄 ${date} 的資產總額。`, true);
});

els.exportBtn.addEventListener("click", async () => {
  const fileName = `stock-ledger-${todayISO()}.json`;
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(state, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });

  if (navigator.canShare && navigator.share) {
    const file = new File([blob], fileName, { type: "application/json" });
    if (navigator.canShare({ files: [file] })) {
      try {
        await navigator.share({ files: [file], title: "股票記帳本資料備份" });
        setStatus("資料已匯出。");
        return;
      } catch (error) {
        if (error?.name === "AbortError") return;
      }
    }
  }

  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = url;
  link.download = fileName;
  link.target = "_blank";
  document.body.append(link);
  link.click();
  link.remove();
  window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
  setStatus("資料已匯出。");
});

els.importBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
  els.importFile.click();
});

els.importFile.addEventListener("change", async () => {
  const file = els.importFile.files?.[0];
  if (!file) return;

  try {
    const imported = migrateState(JSON.parse(await file.text()));
    if (!confirm("匯入會用備份檔覆蓋目前畫面中的資料，確定要匯入？")) return;
    state.holdings = imported.holdings;
    state.usHoldings = imported.usHoldings;
    state.funds = imported.funds;
    state.cryptoContracts = imported.cryptoContracts;
    state.history = imported.history;
    state.tradeHistory = imported.tradeHistory;
    state.currentFundTotal = imported.currentFundTotal;
    state.cash = imported.cash;
    state.usdTwdRate = imported.usdTwdRate;
    state.usCashUsd = imported.usCashUsd;
    els.cash.value = state.cash ? String(state.cash) : "";
    els.usdTwdRate.value = state.usdTwdRate ? String(state.usdTwdRate) : "";
    els.usCashUsd.value = state.usCashUsd ? String(state.usCashUsd) : "";
    saveAndRender("資料已匯入。");
  } catch {
    setStatus("匯入失敗，請確認檔案是本 app 匯出的 JSON。");
  } finally {
    els.importFile.value = "";
  }
});

[els.tradeBuyDate, els.tradeSellDate, els.tradeShares, els.tradeAverageCost, els.tradeSellPrice, els.tradeFxRate]
  .forEach((input) => input.addEventListener("input", updateTradeDecisionPreview));

els.tradeDecisionForm.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
  event.preventDefault();
  recordPendingHoldingSale();
});

els.skipTradeBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
  if (!pendingHoldingSale) return;
  removePendingHolding();
  closeTradeDecision();
  saveAndRender("已刪除持股，未建立交易紀錄。", true);
});

els.cancelTradeBtn.addEventListener("click", closeTradeDecision);
els.tradeDecisionDialog.addEventListener("cancel", () => {
  pendingHoldingSale = null;
  els.tradeDecisionError.textContent = "";
});

els.tradeMarketFilter.addEventListener("change", renderTradeHistory);
els.tradeMonthFilter.addEventListener("change", renderTradeHistory);
els.clearTradeFiltersBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
  els.tradeMarketFilter.value = "all";
  els.tradeMonthFilter.value = "";
  renderTradeHistory();
});
els.exportTradesBtn.addEventListener("click", exportTradeHistoryExcel);

els.tradeHistoryList.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
  const button = event.target.closest("[data-trade-action]");
  if (!button) return;
  const trade = findTradeRecord(button.dataset.tradeId);
  if (!trade) return;

  if (button.dataset.tradeAction === "edit") {
    editingTradeId = trade.id;
    els.tradeHistoryDetails.open = true;
    renderTradeHistory();
    return;
  }

  if (button.dataset.tradeAction === "cancel") {
    editingTradeId = "";
    renderTradeHistory();
    return;
  }

  if (button.dataset.tradeAction === "save") {
    const form = button.closest("[data-trade-edit-form]");
    if (!form) return;
    const updated = readTradeEditForm(form, trade);
    if (!updated) return;
    const index = state.tradeHistory.findIndex((item) => item.id === trade.id);
    if (index < 0) return;
    state.tradeHistory[index] = updated;
    editingTradeId = "";
    saveAndRender("已更新交易紀錄。", true);
    return;
  }

  if (button.dataset.tradeAction === "delete") {
    openDeleteTradeDialog(trade);
  }
});

els.tradeHistoryList.addEventListener("submit", (event) => {
  const form = event.target.closest("[data-trade-edit-form]");
  if (!form) return;
  event.preventDefault();
  const trade = findTradeRecord(form.dataset.tradeEditForm);
  if (!trade) return;
  const updated = readTradeEditForm(form, trade);
  if (!updated) return;
  const index = state.tradeHistory.findIndex((item) => item.id === trade.id);
  if (index < 0) return;
  state.tradeHistory[index] = updated;
  editingTradeId = "";
  saveAndRender("已更新交易紀錄。", true);
});

els.confirmDeleteTradeBtn.addEventListener("click", () => {
  const trade = findTradeRecord(pendingTradeDeleteId);
  if (!trade) {
    closeDeleteTradeDialog();
    return;
  }
  state.tradeHistory = state.tradeHistory.filter((item) => item.id !== trade.id);
  if (editingTradeId === trade.id) editingTradeId = "";
  closeDeleteTradeDialog();
  saveAndRender("已刪除交易紀錄；目前持股沒有變動。", true);
});

els.cancelDeleteTradeBtn.addEventListener("click", closeDeleteTradeDialog);
els.deleteTradeDialog.addEventListener("cancel", () => {
  pendingTradeDeleteId = "";
});

els.historyList.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
  const monthToggle = event.target.closest("[data-history-month-toggle]");
  if (monthToggle) {
    const month = monthToggle.dataset.historyMonthToggle;
    if (openHistoryMonths.has(month)) {
      openHistoryMonths.delete(month);
    } else {
      openHistoryMonths.add(month);
    }
    renderHistory();
    return;
  }

  const button = event.target.closest("[data-history-action]");
  if (!button) return;

  const date = button.dataset.historyDate;
  const action = button.dataset.historyAction;
  const record = state.history.find((item) => item.date === date);
  if (!record) return;

  if (action === "toggle-note") {
    const card = button.closest("[data-history-card]");
    const note = card?.querySelector("[data-history-note]");
    if (!note) return;
    const isOpen = note.hidden;
    note.hidden = !isOpen;
    button.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
    return;
  }

  if (action === "unlock") {
    editingHistoryDate = date;
    openHistoryMonths.add(String(date || "").slice(0, 7));
    renderHistory();
    return;
  }

  if (action === "cancel") {
    editingHistoryDate = "";
    renderHistory();
    return;
  }

  if (action === "delete") {
    if (!confirm(`確定刪除 ${date} 的歷史紀錄？`)) return;
    state.history = state.history.filter((item) => item.date !== date);
    editingHistoryDate = "";
    saveAndRender(`已刪除 ${date} 的歷史紀錄。`);
    return;
  }

  if (action === "save") {
    const card = button.closest("[data-history-card]");
    const nextDate = parseDateInput(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='date']").value) || date;
    if (nextDate !== date && state.history.some((item) => item.date === nextDate)) {
      setStatus(`${nextDate} 已經有紀錄，請先改成其他日期。`);
      return;
    }

    const stockTotal = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='stockTotal']").value);
    const usStockTotal = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='usStockTotal']").value);
    const fundTotal = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='fundTotal']").value);
    const stockCost = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='stockCost']").value);
    const usStockCost = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='usStockCost']").value);
    const fundCost = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='fundCost']").value);
    const cryptoTotal = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='cryptoTotal']").value);
    const cryptoCost = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='cryptoCost']").value);
    const cash = toNumber(card.querySelector("[data-history-field='cash']").value);
    const note = card.querySelector("[data-history-field='note']").value.trim();
    record.date = nextDate;
    record.stockTotal = stockTotal;
    record.usStockTotal = usStockTotal;
    record.usStockMarketValue = usStockTotal - toNumber(record.usCashTwd);
    record.fundTotal = fundTotal;
    record.cash = cash;
    record.cashRecorded = cash > 0;
    record.stockCost = stockCost;
    record.usStockCost = usStockCost;
    record.fundCost = fundCost;
    record.cryptoTotal = cryptoTotal;
    record.cryptoCost = cryptoCost;
    record.total = stockTotal + usStockTotal + fundTotal + cryptoTotal + cash;
    record.cost = stockCost + usStockCost + fundCost + cryptoCost;
    record.pnl = stockTotal + record.usStockMarketValue + fundTotal + cryptoTotal - record.cost;
    record.note = note;
    state.history.sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
    editingHistoryDate = "";
    openHistoryMonths.add(String(nextDate || "").slice(0, 7));
    saveAndRender(`已更新 ${nextDate} 的歷史紀錄。`);
  }
});

window.addEventListener("resize", drawCharts);

function render() {
  renderSummary();
  renderHoldings();
  renderUsHoldings();
  renderFunds();
  renderCryptoContracts();
  renderSettlementRows();
  renderHistory();
  renderTradeHistory();
  drawCharts();
}

function renderSummary() {
  const totals = getPortfolioTotals();
  els.totalValue.textContent = money(totals.value);
  els.cashTotal.textContent = money(totals.cash);
  els.totalCost.textContent = money(totals.cost);
  els.stockTotal.textContent = money(totals.stockTotal);
  els.stockCost.textContent = money(totals.stockCost);
  els.usStockTotal.textContent = money(totals.usStockTotal);
  els.usStockCost.textContent = money(totals.usStockCost);
  els.fundTotal.textContent = money(totals.fundTotal);
  els.fundCost.textContent = money(totals.fundCost);
  els.cryptoTotal.textContent = money(totals.cryptoTotal);
  els.cryptoCost.textContent = money(totals.cryptoCost);
  els.stockPnl.textContent = signedMoney(totals.stockPnl);
  els.stockPnlPct.textContent = signedPercent(totals.stockPnlPct);
  els.usStockPnl.textContent = signedMoney(totals.usStockPnl);
  els.usStockPnlPct.textContent = signedPercent(totals.usStockPnlPct);
  els.fundPnl.textContent = signedMoney(totals.fundPnl);
  els.fundPnlPct.textContent = signedPercent(totals.fundPnlPct);
  els.cryptoPnl.textContent = signedMoney(totals.cryptoPnl);
  els.cryptoPnlPct.textContent = signedPercent(totals.cryptoPnlPct);
  els.totalPnl.textContent = signedMoney(totals.pnl);
  els.totalPnlPct.textContent = signedPercent(totals.pnlPct);
  els.stockPnl.className = totals.stockPnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.stockPnlPct.className = totals.stockPnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.usStockPnl.className = totals.usStockPnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.usStockPnlPct.className = totals.usStockPnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.fundPnl.className = totals.fundPnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.fundPnlPct.className = totals.fundPnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.cryptoPnl.className = totals.cryptoPnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.cryptoPnlPct.className = totals.cryptoPnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.totalPnl.className = totals.pnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.totalPnlPct.className = totals.pnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
}

function renderHoldings() {
  els.holdingsBody.innerHTML = "";

  if (!state.holdings.length) {
    els.holdingsBody.append(els.emptyTemplate.content.cloneNode(true));
    return;
  }

  const sortedHoldings = [...state.holdings].sort(compareHoldingsByDisplayName);

  for (const holding of sortedHoldings) {
    const price = holding.currentPrice ?? 0;
    const marketValue = holding.shares * price;
    const costValue = holding.shares * holding.avgCost;
    const pnl = marketValue - costValue;
    const pnlPct = costValue ? (pnl / costValue) * 100 : 0;
    const row = document.createElement("tr");
    row.innerHTML = `
      <td>
        <strong>${escapeHTML(holding.name || holding.symbol)}</strong>
        <span class="stock-name">${escapeHTML(holding.symbol)}</span>
      </td>
      <td class="shares-cell">${formatNumber(holding.shares, 3)}</td>
      <td class="cost-cell">${unitMoney(holding.avgCost)}</td>
      <td class="price-cell">
        <input data-price-id="${holding.id}" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${holding.currentPrice ?? ""}" aria-label="${escapeHTML(holding.symbol)} 現價" />
      </td>
      <td class="amount-cell">${money(costValue)}</td>
      <td class="amount-cell">${money(marketValue)}</td>
      <td class="pnl-cell ${pnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss"}">
        ${signedMoney(pnl)}
        <span class="stock-name">${formatNumber(pnlPct)}%</span>
      </td>
      <td>
        <div class="row-actions">
          <button class="mini-btn" data-edit-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="button" aria-label="修改 ${escapeHTML(holding.symbol)}">${editIcon()}</button>
          <button class="mini-btn" data-delete-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="button" aria-label="刪除 ${escapeHTML(holding.symbol)}">x</button>
        </div>
      </td>
    `;
    els.holdingsBody.append(row);

    if (editingHoldingId === holding.id) {
      const editRow = document.createElement("tr");
      editRow.className = "edit-holding-row";
      editRow.dataset.editRow = holding.id;
      editRow.innerHTML = `
        <td colspan="8">
          <div class="edit-holding-form">
            <label>
              中文名
              <input data-edit-field="name" autocomplete="off" value="${escapeHTML(holding.name || "")}" />
            </label>
            <label>
              股號
              <input data-edit-field="symbol" autocomplete="off" value="${escapeHTML(holding.symbol || "")}" />
            </label>
            <label>
              張數
              <input data-edit-field="shares" type="number" min="0" step="0.001" value="${holding.shares}" />
            </label>
            <label>
              買入價格
              <input data-edit-field="avgCost" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${holding.avgCost}" />
            </label>
            <label>
              買入日期
              <input data-edit-field="purchaseDate" type="date" value="${escapeHTML(holding.purchaseDate || LEGACY_PURCHASE_DATE)}" />
            </label>
            <button class="primary-btn" data-edit-save-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="button">儲存</button>
            <button class="secondary-btn" data-edit-cancel-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="button">取消</button>
          </div>
        </td>
      `;
      els.holdingsBody.append(editRow);
    }
  }
}

function renderUsHoldings() {
  els.usHoldingsBody.innerHTML = "";
  if (!state.usHoldings.length) {
    const row = document.createElement("tr");
    row.innerHTML = '<td colspan="8" class="empty-cell">尚未新增美股</td>';
    els.usHoldingsBody.append(row);
    return;
  }

  for (const holding of [...state.usHoldings].sort(compareHoldingsByDisplayName)) {
    const price = holding.currentPrice ?? 0;
    const fxRate = state.usdTwdRate || 0;
    const costValue = holding.shares * holding.avgCost * fxRate;
    const marketValue = holding.shares * price * fxRate;
    const pnl = marketValue - costValue;
    const pnlPct = costValue ? (pnl / costValue) * 100 : 0;
    const row = document.createElement("tr");
    row.innerHTML = `
      <td><strong>${escapeHTML(holding.symbol)}</strong></td>
      <td class="shares-cell">${formatNumber(holding.shares, 3)}</td>
      <td class="cost-cell">${usdMoney(holding.avgCost)}</td>
      <td class="price-cell"><input data-us-price-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${holding.currentPrice ?? ""}" aria-label="${escapeHTML(holding.symbol)} 現價" /></td>
      <td class="amount-cell">${money(costValue)}</td>
      <td class="amount-cell">${money(marketValue)}</td>
      <td class="pnl-cell ${pnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss"}">${signedMoney(pnl)}<span class="stock-name">${signedPercent(pnlPct)}</span></td>
      <td><div class="row-actions"><button class="mini-btn" data-edit-us-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="button" aria-label="修改 ${escapeHTML(holding.symbol)}">${editIcon()}</button><button class="mini-btn" data-delete-us-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="button" aria-label="刪除 ${escapeHTML(holding.symbol)}">x</button></div></td>
    `;
    els.usHoldingsBody.append(row);

    if (editingUsHoldingId === holding.id) {
      const editRow = document.createElement("tr");
      editRow.className = "edit-holding-row";
      editRow.dataset.editUsRow = holding.id;
      editRow.innerHTML = `<td colspan="8"><div class="edit-holding-form us-edit-holding-form">
        <label>代號<input data-edit-us-field="symbol" autocomplete="off" value="${escapeHTML(holding.symbol || "")}" /></label>
        <label>股數<input data-edit-us-field="shares" type="number" min="0" step="0.001" value="${holding.shares}" /></label>
        <label>平均成本 USD<input data-edit-us-field="avgCost" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${holding.avgCost}" /></label>
        <label>買入日期<input data-edit-us-field="purchaseDate" type="date" value="${escapeHTML(holding.purchaseDate || LEGACY_PURCHASE_DATE)}" /></label>
        <button class="primary-btn" data-edit-us-save-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="button">儲存</button>
        <button class="secondary-btn" data-edit-us-cancel-id="${escapeHTML(holding.id)}" type="button">取消</button>
      </div></td>`;
      els.usHoldingsBody.append(editRow);
    }
  }
}

function renderFunds() {
  els.fundsBody.innerHTML = "";

  if (!state.funds.length) {
    const row = document.createElement("tr");
    row.innerHTML = '<td colspan="5" class="empty-cell">尚未新增基金</td>';
    els.fundsBody.append(row);
    return;
  }

  for (const fund of state.funds) {
    const pnl = (fund.currentValue || 0) - (fund.cost || 0);
    const pnlPct = fund.cost ? (pnl / fund.cost) * 100 : 0;
    const row = document.createElement("tr");
    row.innerHTML = `
      <td>
        <strong>${escapeHTML(fund.name)}</strong>
      </td>
      <td class="fund-input-cell">
        <input data-fund-cost-id="${fund.id}" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${fund.cost ?? 0}" aria-label="${escapeHTML(fund.name)} 基金成本" />
      </td>
      <td class="fund-input-cell">
        <input data-fund-value-id="${fund.id}" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${fund.currentValue ?? 0}" aria-label="${escapeHTML(fund.name)} 目前總額" />
      </td>
      <td class="fund-pnl-cell ${pnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss"}">
        <div class="fund-bottom-line">
          <div class="fund-pnl-line">
            <span>${signedMoney(pnl)}</span>
            <span class="stock-name">${signedPercent(pnlPct)}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="row-actions">
            <button class="mini-btn" data-delete-fund-id="${escapeHTML(fund.id)}" type="button" aria-label="刪除 ${escapeHTML(fund.name)}">x</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </td>
      <td class="fund-actions-cell"></td>
    `;
    els.fundsBody.append(row);

    if (editingFundId === fund.id) {
      const editRow = document.createElement("tr");
      editRow.className = "edit-fund-row";
      editRow.dataset.editFundRow = fund.id;
      editRow.innerHTML = `
        <td colspan="5">
          <div class="edit-fund-form">
            <label>
              基金名稱
              <input data-edit-fund-field="name" autocomplete="off" value="${escapeHTML(fund.name || "")}" />
            </label>
            <label>
              基金成本
              <input data-edit-fund-field="cost" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${fund.cost ?? 0}" />
            </label>
            <label>
              目前總額
              <input data-edit-fund-field="currentValue" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${fund.currentValue ?? 0}" />
            </label>
            <button class="primary-btn" data-edit-fund-save-id="${escapeHTML(fund.id)}" type="button">儲存</button>
            <button class="secondary-btn" data-edit-fund-cancel-id="${escapeHTML(fund.id)}" type="button">取消</button>
          </div>
        </td>
      `;
      els.fundsBody.append(editRow);
    }
  }
}

function renderCryptoContracts() {
  els.cryptoList.innerHTML = "";

  if (!state.cryptoContracts.length) {
    const empty = document.createElement("p");
    empty.className = "status-text";
    empty.textContent = "尚未新增加密貨幣合約";
    els.cryptoList.append(empty);
    return;
  }

  for (const contract of state.cryptoContracts) {
    const position = calculateCryptoPosition(contract);
    const baseAsset = getCryptoBaseAsset(contract.name);
    const card = document.createElement("details");
    card.className = "crypto-card";
    card.open = openCryptoCards.has(contract.id);
    card.innerHTML = `
      <summary class="crypto-card-toggle">
        <strong>${escapeHTML(contract.name)}</strong>
        <span class="crypto-card-toggle-pnl ${position.pnlTwd >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss"}">
          <span>${signedMoney(position.pnlTwd)}</span>
          <span>${signedPercent(position.pnlPct)}</span>
        </span>
      </summary>
      <div class="crypto-card-content">
      <div class="crypto-card-fields">
      <label>
        方向
        <select data-crypto-id="${escapeHTML(contract.id)}" data-crypto-field="side">
          <option value="long"${contract.side === "long" ? " selected" : ""}>做多</option>
          <option value="short"${contract.side === "short" ? " selected" : ""}>做空</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <label>
        合約倍數
        <input data-crypto-id="${escapeHTML(contract.id)}" data-crypto-field="leverage" type="number" min="1" step="1" value="${contract.leverage}" />
      </label>
      <label>
        保證金 USDT
        <input data-crypto-id="${escapeHTML(contract.id)}" data-crypto-field="marginUsdt" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${contract.marginUsdt}" />
      </label>
      <label>
        ${escapeHTML(baseAsset)}持倉數量
        <input data-crypto-id="${escapeHTML(contract.id)}" data-crypto-field="quantity" type="number" min="0" step="0.000001" value="${contract.quantity}" />
      </label>
      <label>
        開倉價
        <input data-crypto-id="${escapeHTML(contract.id)}" data-crypto-field="entryPrice" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${contract.entryPrice}" />
      </label>
      <label>
        目前價
        <input data-crypto-id="${escapeHTML(contract.id)}" data-crypto-field="currentPrice" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${contract.currentPrice}" />
      </label>
      </div>
      <div class="crypto-position-details">
        <span>開倉部位 <strong>${formatNumber(position.notionalUsdt, 2)}</strong></span>
        <span>${escapeHTML(baseAsset)} 數量 <strong>${formatNumber(position.quantity, 6)}</strong></span>
        <span>未實現損益 <strong>${signedNumber(position.pnlUsdt, 2)}</strong></span>
        <span>目前權益 <strong>${formatNumber(position.equityUsdt, 2)}</strong></span>
      </div>
      <div class="crypto-card-bottom">
        <div class="crypto-card-metrics">
          <div class="crypto-cost-metric">
            <span class="crypto-pnl-label">成本</span>
            <span>${money(position.costTwd)}</span>
          </div>
          <div class="crypto-pnl-metric ${position.pnlTwd >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss"}">
            <span class="crypto-pnl-label">損益</span>
            <div class="fund-pnl-line">
              <span>${signedMoney(position.pnlTwd)}</span>
              <span class="stock-name">${signedPercent(position.pnlPct)}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="fund-bottom-line">
          <div class="row-actions">
            <button class="mini-btn" data-delete-crypto-id="${escapeHTML(contract.id)}" type="button" aria-label="刪除 ${escapeHTML(contract.name)}">x</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      </div>
    `;
    card.addEventListener("toggle", () => {
      if (card.open) openCryptoCards.add(contract.id);
      else openCryptoCards.delete(contract.id);
    });
    els.cryptoList.append(card);
  }
}

function renderSettlementRows() {
  els.settlementRows.innerHTML = "";

  if (!state.holdings.length && !state.usHoldings.length && !state.funds.length && !state.cryptoContracts.length && !state.cash && !state.usCashUsd) {
    const empty = document.createElement("p");
    empty.className = "status-text";
    empty.textContent = "尚未新增台股、美股、基金、加密貨幣合約或現金水位";
    els.settlementRows.append(empty);
    return;
  }

  const totals = getPortfolioTotals();
  const summary = document.createElement("div");
  summary.className = "settlement-summary";
  summary.innerHTML = `
    <div>
      <span>現金</span>
      <strong>${money(totals.cash)}</strong>
    </div>
    <div>
      <span>台灣股市</span>
      <strong>${money(totals.stockTotal)}</strong>
    </div>
    <div>
      <span>基金</span>
      <strong>${money(totals.fundTotal)}</strong>
    </div>
    <div>
      <span>加密</span>
      <strong>${money(totals.cryptoTotal)}</strong>
    </div>
    <div>
      <span>美國股市</span>
      <strong>${money(totals.usStockTotal)}</strong>
    </div>
    <div>
      <span>損益</span>
      <strong class="${totals.pnl >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss"}">${signedMoney(totals.pnl)}</strong>
    </div>
  `;
  els.settlementRows.append(summary);
}

function openTradeDecision(market, holding) {
  pendingHoldingSale = { market, holdingId: holding.id };
  const isUs = market === "us";
  els.tradeDecisionStock.textContent = getTradeDisplayLabel({ market, name: holding.name, symbol: holding.symbol });
  els.tradeBuyDate.value = holding.purchaseDate || LEGACY_PURCHASE_DATE;
  els.tradeSellDate.value = todayISO();
  els.tradeShares.value = String(holding.shares || "");
  els.tradeAverageCost.value = String(holding.avgCost ?? "");
  els.tradeSellPrice.value = String(holding.currentPrice ?? holding.avgCost ?? "");
  els.tradeFxRate.value = String(isUs ? (state.usdTwdRate || "") : 1);
  els.tradeFxRateField.hidden = !isUs;
  els.tradeFxRate.disabled = !isUs;
  els.tradeFxRate.required = isUs;
  els.tradeAverageCostLabel.textContent = isUs ? "平均成本 USD" : "平均成本";
  els.tradeSellPriceLabel.textContent = isUs ? "賣出價格 USD" : "賣出價格";
  els.tradeDecisionError.textContent = "";
  updateTradeDecisionPreview();
  if (!els.tradeDecisionDialog.open) els.tradeDecisionDialog.showModal();
  els.tradeDecisionDialog.scrollTop = 0;
  window.requestAnimationFrame(() => els.tradeDecisionDialog.focus({ preventScroll: true }));
}

function closeTradeDecision() {
  pendingHoldingSale = null;
  els.tradeDecisionError.textContent = "";
  if (els.tradeDecisionDialog.open) els.tradeDecisionDialog.close();
}

function getTradeDecisionValues() {
  return {
    buyDate: parseDateInput(els.tradeBuyDate.value),
    sellDate: parseDateInput(els.tradeSellDate.value),
    shares: toNumber(els.tradeShares.value),
    avgCost: toNumber(els.tradeAverageCost.value),
    sellPrice: toNumber(els.tradeSellPrice.value),
    fxRate: pendingHoldingSale?.market === "us" ? toNumber(els.tradeFxRate.value) : 1,
  };
}

function updateTradeDecisionPreview() {
  const market = pendingHoldingSale?.market || "tw";
  const values = getTradeDecisionValues();
  const calculations = calculateTradeValues(market, values.shares, values.avgCost, values.sellPrice, values.fxRate);
  els.tradePreviewCost.textContent = money(calculations.costTwd);
  els.tradePreviewProceeds.textContent = money(calculations.proceedsTwd);
  els.tradePreviewPnl.textContent = signedMoney(calculations.pnlTwd);
  els.tradePreviewPnlPct.textContent = signedPercent(calculations.pnlPct);
  els.tradePreviewPnl.className = calculations.pnlTwd >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
  els.tradePreviewPnlPct.className = calculations.pnlTwd >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss";
}

function recordPendingHoldingSale() {
  if (!pendingHoldingSale) return;
  const holding = pendingHoldingSale.market === "us"
    ? findUsHolding(pendingHoldingSale.holdingId)
    : findHolding(pendingHoldingSale.holdingId);
  if (!holding) {
    els.tradeDecisionError.textContent = "找不到這筆持股，請關閉後重試。";
    return;
  }

  const values = getTradeDecisionValues();
  const error = validateTradeValues(pendingHoldingSale.market, values);
  if (error) {
    els.tradeDecisionError.textContent = error;
    return;
  }

  const trade = createTradeRecord({
    market: pendingHoldingSale.market,
    symbol: holding.symbol,
    name: holding.name || "",
    ...values,
  });
  state.tradeHistory.push(trade);
  removePendingHolding();
  closeTradeDecision();
  els.tradeHistoryDetails.open = true;
  saveAndRender("已建立交易紀錄並刪除持股。", true);
}

function removePendingHolding() {
  if (!pendingHoldingSale) return;
  const { market, holdingId } = pendingHoldingSale;
  if (market === "us") {
    state.usHoldings = state.usHoldings.filter((item) => item.id !== holdingId);
    if (editingUsHoldingId === holdingId) editingUsHoldingId = "";
  } else {
    state.holdings = state.holdings.filter((item) => item.id !== holdingId);
    if (editingHoldingId === holdingId) editingHoldingId = "";
  }
}

function validateTradeValues(market, values) {
  if (!values.buyDate || !values.sellDate) return "請確認買入日期與賣出日期。";
  if (values.sellDate < values.buyDate) return "賣出日期不能早於買入日期。";
  if (!(values.shares > 0)) return "股數必須大於 0。";
  if (values.avgCost < 0 || values.sellPrice < 0) return "成本與賣出價格不能小於 0。";
  if (market === "us" && !(values.fxRate > 0)) return "美股交易請填寫有效的賣出匯率。";
  return "";
}

function calculateTradeValues(market, shares, avgCost, sellPrice, fxRate) {
  const safeShares = Math.max(0, toNumber(shares));
  const safeAverageCost = Math.max(0, toNumber(avgCost));
  const safeSellPrice = Math.max(0, toNumber(sellPrice));
  const safeFxRate = market === "us" ? Math.max(0, toNumber(fxRate)) : 1;
  const costNative = safeShares * safeAverageCost;
  const proceedsNative = safeShares * safeSellPrice;
  const pnlNative = proceedsNative - costNative;
  const costTwd = costNative * safeFxRate;
  const proceedsTwd = proceedsNative * safeFxRate;
  const pnlTwd = proceedsTwd - costTwd;
  const pnlPct = costNative ? (pnlNative / costNative) * 100 : 0;
  return { costNative, proceedsNative, pnlNative, costTwd, proceedsTwd, pnlTwd, pnlPct };
}

function createTradeRecord(values) {
  const market = values.market === "us" ? "us" : "tw";
  const calculations = calculateTradeValues(market, values.shares, values.avgCost, values.sellPrice, values.fxRate);
  return {
    id: values.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
    market,
    symbol: normalizeSymbol(String(values.symbol || "")),
    name: String(values.name || "").trim(),
    buyDate: parseDateInput(values.buyDate) || LEGACY_PURCHASE_DATE,
    sellDate: parseDateInput(values.sellDate) || todayISO(),
    shares: Math.max(0, toNumber(values.shares)),
    avgCost: Math.max(0, toNumber(values.avgCost)),
    sellPrice: Math.max(0, toNumber(values.sellPrice)),
    fxRate: market === "us" ? Math.max(0, toNumber(values.fxRate)) : 1,
    currency: market === "us" ? "USD" : "TWD",
    ...calculations,
    recordedAt: String(values.recordedAt || new Date().toISOString()),
  };
}

function renderTradeHistory() {
  const allTrades = [...state.tradeHistory].sort(compareTradeRecords);
  const totalCost = allTrades.reduce((sum, trade) => sum + trade.costTwd, 0);
  const totalPnl = allTrades.reduce((sum, trade) => sum + trade.pnlTwd, 0);
  const totalPnlPct = totalCost ? (totalPnl / totalCost) * 100 : 0;
  els.tradeHistorySummary.textContent = allTrades.length
    ? `${allTrades.length} 筆 · ${signedMoney(totalPnl)} · ${signedPercent(totalPnlPct)}`
    : "尚無交易紀錄";
  els.exportTradesBtn.disabled = allTrades.length === 0;

  const marketFilter = els.tradeMarketFilter.value || "all";
  const monthFilter = els.tradeMonthFilter.value || "";
  const visibleTrades = allTrades.filter((trade) =>
    (marketFilter === "all" || trade.market === marketFilter)
    && (!monthFilter || trade.sellDate.startsWith(monthFilter))
  );

  els.tradeHistoryList.innerHTML = "";
  if (!visibleTrades.length) {
    const empty = document.createElement("p");
    empty.className = "trade-record-empty";
    empty.textContent = allTrades.length ? "目前篩選條件沒有交易紀錄。" : "刪除持股並選擇「是，紀錄」後，交易會顯示在這裡。";
    els.tradeHistoryList.append(empty);
    return;
  }

  for (const trade of visibleTrades) {
    const record = document.createElement("article");
    record.className = "trade-record";
    record.dataset.tradeRecord = trade.id;
    if (editingTradeId === trade.id) {
      record.classList.add("is-editing");
      record.innerHTML = renderTradeEditForm(trade);
    } else {
      record.innerHTML = renderTradeRecord(trade);
    }
    els.tradeHistoryList.append(record);
  }
}

function compareTradeRecords(a, b) {
  return String(b.sellDate).localeCompare(String(a.sellDate))
    || String(b.recordedAt).localeCompare(String(a.recordedAt))
    || String(a.symbol).localeCompare(String(b.symbol));
}

function renderTradeRecord(trade) {
  const averageCost = trade.market === "us" ? usdMoney(trade.avgCost) : unitMoney(trade.avgCost);
  const sellPrice = trade.market === "us" ? usdMoney(trade.sellPrice) : unitMoney(trade.sellPrice);
  const costNative = trade.market === "us" ? `<span class="trade-record-native">${usdMoney(trade.costNative)}</span>` : "";
  const proceedsNative = trade.market === "us" ? `<span class="trade-record-native">${usdMoney(trade.proceedsNative)}</span>` : "";
  const pnlNative = trade.market === "us" ? `<span class="trade-record-native">${signedUsdMoney(trade.pnlNative)}</span>` : "";
  return `
    <span class="trade-record-field">
      <small>市場／股票</small>
      <span class="trade-record-symbol">${escapeHTML(getTradeDisplayLabel(trade))}</span>
    </span>
    <span class="trade-record-field"><small>買入日</small>${escapeHTML(trade.buyDate)}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-field"><small>賣出日</small>${escapeHTML(trade.sellDate)}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-field"><small>股數</small>${formatNumber(trade.shares, 3)}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-field"><small>平均成本</small>${averageCost}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-field"><small>賣出價</small>${sellPrice}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-field"><small>總成本</small>${money(trade.costTwd)}${costNative}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-field"><small>賣出金額</small>${money(trade.proceedsTwd)}${proceedsNative}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-field ${trade.pnlTwd >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss"}"><small>獲利</small>${signedMoney(trade.pnlTwd)}${pnlNative}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-field ${trade.pnlTwd >= 0 ? "gain" : "loss"}"><small>獲利%</small>${signedPercent(trade.pnlPct)}</span>
    <span class="trade-record-actions">
      <button class="mini-btn" data-trade-action="edit" data-trade-id="${escapeHTML(trade.id)}" type="button" aria-label="修改 ${escapeHTML(trade.symbol)} 交易紀錄">${editIcon()}</button>
      <button class="mini-btn" data-trade-action="delete" data-trade-id="${escapeHTML(trade.id)}" type="button" aria-label="刪除 ${escapeHTML(trade.symbol)} 交易紀錄">x</button>
    </span>
  `;
}

function renderTradeEditForm(trade) {
  return `
    <form class="trade-edit-form" data-trade-edit-form="${escapeHTML(trade.id)}">
      <label>市場<select data-trade-field="market"><option value="tw"${trade.market === "tw" ? " selected" : ""}>台股</option><option value="us"${trade.market === "us" ? " selected" : ""}>美股</option></select></label>
      <label>股票代號<input data-trade-field="symbol" autocomplete="off" value="${escapeHTML(trade.symbol)}" required /></label>
      <label>股票名稱<input data-trade-field="name" autocomplete="off" value="${escapeHTML(trade.name)}" /></label>
      <label>買入日期<input data-trade-field="buyDate" type="date" value="${escapeHTML(trade.buyDate)}" required /></label>
      <label>賣出日期<input data-trade-field="sellDate" type="date" value="${escapeHTML(trade.sellDate)}" required /></label>
      <label>股數<input data-trade-field="shares" type="number" min="0.000001" step="any" value="${trade.shares}" required /></label>
      <label>平均成本<input data-trade-field="avgCost" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${trade.avgCost}" required /></label>
      <label>賣出價格<input data-trade-field="sellPrice" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" value="${trade.sellPrice}" required /></label>
      <label>美股賣出匯率<input data-trade-field="fxRate" type="number" min="0.001" step="0.001" value="${trade.fxRate}" /></label>
      <p class="trade-dialog-error" data-trade-edit-error></p>
      <div class="trade-edit-actions">
        <button class="primary-btn" data-trade-action="save" data-trade-id="${escapeHTML(trade.id)}" type="button">儲存</button>
        <button class="secondary-btn" data-trade-action="cancel" data-trade-id="${escapeHTML(trade.id)}" type="button">取消</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  `;
}

function readTradeEditForm(form, trade) {
  const market = form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='market']").value === "us" ? "us" : "tw";
  const values = {
    id: trade.id,
    market,
    symbol: normalizeSymbol(form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='symbol']").value),
    name: form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='name']").value.trim(),
    buyDate: parseDateInput(form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='buyDate']").value),
    sellDate: parseDateInput(form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='sellDate']").value),
    shares: toNumber(form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='shares']").value),
    avgCost: toNumber(form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='avgCost']").value),
    sellPrice: toNumber(form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='sellPrice']").value),
    fxRate: market === "us" ? toNumber(form.querySelector("[data-trade-field='fxRate']").value) : 1,
    recordedAt: trade.recordedAt,
  };
  const errorNode = form.querySelector("[data-trade-edit-error]");
  const error = !values.symbol ? "請填寫股票代號。" : validateTradeValues(market, values);
  errorNode.textContent = error;
  return error ? null : createTradeRecord(values);
}

function openDeleteTradeDialog(trade) {
  pendingTradeDeleteId = trade.id;
  els.deleteTradeDescription.textContent = `${getTradeDisplayLabel(trade)} · ${trade.buyDate} → ${trade.sellDate} · ${signedMoney(trade.pnlTwd)}`;
  if (!els.deleteTradeDialog.open) els.deleteTradeDialog.showModal();
}

function closeDeleteTradeDialog() {
  pendingTradeDeleteId = "";
  if (els.deleteTradeDialog.open) els.deleteTradeDialog.close();
}

function findTradeRecord(id) {
  return state.tradeHistory.find((item) => item.id === id);
}

function getTradeDisplayLabel(trade) {
  const symbol = normalizeSymbol(String(trade.symbol || ""));
  const name = String(trade.name || "").trim();
  if (trade.market === "us") return `美股 / ${name || symbol}`;
  return `台股 / ${[name, symbol].filter(Boolean).join(" / ")}`;
}

function downloadBlob(blob, fileName) {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.href = url;
  link.download = fileName;
  link.style.display = "none";
  document.body.append(link);
  link.click();
  window.setTimeout(() => {
    link.remove();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, 1000);
}

function showTradeExportFeedback(message, delay = 2400) {
  window.clearTimeout(tradeExportFeedbackTimer);
  els.exportTradesBtn.textContent = message;
  tradeExportFeedbackTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
    els.exportTradesBtn.textContent = "匯出全部 Excel";
  }, delay);
}

function exportTradeHistoryExcel() {
  const trades = [...state.tradeHistory].sort(compareTradeRecords).reverse();
  if (!trades.length) {
    setStatus("目前沒有可匯出的交易紀錄。");
    return;
  }
  if (!window.XLSX) {
    setStatus("Excel 匯出元件未載入，請重新整理後再試。" );
    return;
  }

  setStatus("正在產生交易紀錄 Excel...");
  showTradeExportFeedback("正在產生...");
  try {
    const headers = ["市場", "股票代號", "股票名稱", "買入日期", "賣出日期", "股數", "幣別", "平均成本", "賣出價格", "賣出匯率", "原幣成本", "原幣賣出金額", "原幣獲利", "台幣成本", "台幣賣出金額", "台幣獲利", "獲利%"];
    const rows = trades.map((trade) => [
      trade.market === "us" ? "美股" : "台股",
      trade.symbol,
      trade.name,
      trade.buyDate,
      trade.sellDate,
      trade.shares,
      trade.currency,
      trade.avgCost,
      trade.sellPrice,
      trade.fxRate,
      trade.costNative,
      trade.proceedsNative,
      trade.pnlNative,
      trade.costTwd,
      trade.proceedsTwd,
      trade.pnlTwd,
      trade.pnlPct / 100,
    ]);
    const totalCost = trades.reduce((sum, trade) => sum + trade.costTwd, 0);
    const totalProceeds = trades.reduce((sum, trade) => sum + trade.proceedsTwd, 0);
    const totalPnl = trades.reduce((sum, trade) => sum + trade.pnlTwd, 0);
    const totalPnlPct = totalCost ? totalPnl / totalCost : 0;
    const totalRow = ["合計", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", totalCost, totalProceeds, totalPnl, totalPnlPct];
    const worksheet = window.XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet([headers, ...rows, [], totalRow]);
    worksheet["!cols"] = [
      { wch: 8 }, { wch: 13 }, { wch: 18 }, { wch: 12 }, { wch: 12 }, { wch: 12 },
      { wch: 8 }, { wch: 13 }, { wch: 13 }, { wch: 11 }, { wch: 15 }, { wch: 16 },
      { wch: 15 }, { wch: 15 }, { wch: 16 }, { wch: 15 }, { wch: 11 },
    ];
    worksheet["!autofilter"] = { ref: `A1:Q${rows.length + 1}` };
    for (let row = 2; row <= rows.length + 1; row += 1) {
      ["F", "H", "I", "J", "K", "L", "M", "N", "O", "P"].forEach((column) => {
        if (worksheet[`${column}${row}`]) worksheet[`${column}${row}`].z = "#,##0.00";
      });
      if (worksheet[`Q${row}`]) worksheet[`Q${row}`].z = "0.00%";
    }
    const totalRowNumber = rows.length + 3;
    ["N", "O", "P"].forEach((column) => {
      if (worksheet[`${column}${totalRowNumber}`]) worksheet[`${column}${totalRowNumber}`].z = "#,##0.00";
    });
    if (worksheet[`Q${totalRowNumber}`]) worksheet[`Q${totalRowNumber}`].z = "0.00%";

    const workbook = window.XLSX.utils.book_new();
    window.XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(workbook, worksheet, "交易紀錄");
    const workbookData = window.XLSX.write(workbook, { bookType: "xlsx", type: "array", compression: true });
    const fileName = `stock-trades-${todayISO()}.xlsx`;
    const blob = new Blob([workbookData], { type: "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet" });
    downloadBlob(blob, fileName);
    setStatus(`已開始下載 ${fileName}。`);
    showTradeExportFeedback("Excel 已下載 ✓");
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    setStatus("Excel 匯出失敗，請重新整理後再試。");
    showTradeExportFeedback("匯出失敗", 3200);
  }
}

const historyExtremaMetrics = [
  { key: "total", label: "總資金", getValue: (item) => toNumber(item.total) },
  { key: "cash", label: "現金", getValue: (item) => getRecordedCash(item) },
  { key: "stockTotal", label: "台股", getValue: (item) => toNumber(item.stockTotal) },
  { key: "fundTotal", label: "基金", getValue: (item) => toNumber(item.fundTotal) },
  { key: "cryptoTotal", label: "加密", getValue: (item) => toNumber(item.cryptoTotal) },
  { key: "usStockTotal", label: "美股", getValue: (item) => toNumber(item.usStockTotal) },
];

function getHistoryExtrema(historyItems) {
  const extrema = {};

  for (const metric of historyExtremaMetrics) {
    let high = null;
    let low = null;

    for (const item of historyItems) {
      const value = metric.getValue(item);
      if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value === 0) continue;
      if (!high || value > high.value) high = { value, date: item.date };
      if (!low || value < low.value) low = { value, date: item.date };
    }

    if (high && low) extrema[metric.key] = { high, low };
  }

  return extrema;
}

function getHistoryExtremaClasses(extrema, key, date) {
  const classes = [];
  if (extrema[key]?.high.date === date) classes.push("is-ath");
  if (extrema[key]?.low.date === date) classes.push("is-atl");
  return classes.join(" ");
}

function getHistoryExtremaBadges(extrema, date) {
  const highLabels = historyExtremaMetrics
    .filter((metric) => extrema[metric.key]?.high.date === date)
    .map((metric) => metric.label);
  const lowLabels = historyExtremaMetrics
    .filter((metric) => extrema[metric.key]?.low.date === date)
    .map((metric) => metric.label);

  if (!highLabels.length && !lowLabels.length) return "";
  return `
    <span class="history-extrema-badges">
      ${highLabels.length ? `<span class="history-extrema-badge is-ath" title="ATH：${escapeHTML(highLabels.join("、"))}" aria-label="ATH：${escapeHTML(highLabels.join("、"))}">H</span>` : ""}
      ${lowLabels.length ? `<span class="history-extrema-badge is-atl" title="ATL：${escapeHTML(lowLabels.join("、"))}" aria-label="ATL：${escapeHTML(lowLabels.join("、"))}">L</span>` : ""}
    </span>
  `;
}

function renderHistory() {
  els.historyList.innerHTML = "";
  const historyItems = [...state.history].sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
  const historyExtrema = getHistoryExtrema(historyItems);
  const monthGroups = new Map();
  const monthLists = new Map();

  for (const item of historyItems) {
    const month = String(item.date || "").slice(0, 7) || "未分類";
    const monthItems = monthGroups.get(month) || [];
    monthItems.push(item);
    monthGroups.set(month, monthItems);
  }

  const monthTabs = document.createElement("div");
  monthTabs.className = "history-month-tabs";
  monthTabs.setAttribute("role", "tablist");
  els.historyList.append(monthTabs);

  for (const [month] of monthGroups) {
    const isOpen = openHistoryMonths.has(month) || Boolean(editingHistoryDate && editingHistoryDate.startsWith(month));
    const monthButton = document.createElement("button");
    monthButton.type = "button";
    monthButton.className = `history-month-tab${isOpen ? " is-active" : ""}`;
    monthButton.dataset.historyMonthToggle = month;
    monthButton.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(isOpen));
    monthButton.setAttribute("aria-controls", `history-month-${month}`);
    monthButton.textContent = month;
    monthTabs.append(monthButton);

    const monthPanel = document.createElement("div");
    monthPanel.id = `history-month-${month}`;
    monthPanel.className = "history-month-panel history-month-list";
    monthPanel.hidden = !isOpen;
    monthPanel.innerHTML = `<div class="history-month-heading">${escapeHTML(month)}</div>`;
    els.historyList.append(monthPanel);
    monthLists.set(month, monthPanel);
  }

  for (const [index, item] of historyItems.entries()) {
    const node = document.createElement("div");
    node.className = "history-item";
    node.dataset.historyCard = item.date;

    if (editingHistoryDate === item.date) {
      node.classList.add("is-editing");
      node.innerHTML = `
        <label>
          日期
          <input data-history-field="date" type="text" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" value="${escapeHTML(formatDateForInput(item.date))}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          現金
          <input data-history-field="cash" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.cash)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          台灣股市總和
          <input data-history-field="stockTotal" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.stockTotal)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          台灣股市成本
          <input data-history-field="stockCost" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.stockCost)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          基金總和
          <input data-history-field="fundTotal" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.fundTotal)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          基金成本
          <input data-history-field="fundCost" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.fundCost)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          加密總和
          <input data-history-field="cryptoTotal" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.cryptoTotal)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          加密成本
          <input data-history-field="cryptoCost" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.cryptoCost)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          美國股市總和
          <input data-history-field="usStockTotal" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.usStockTotal)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          美國股市成本
          <input data-history-field="usStockCost" type="number" min="0" step="1" value="${toNumber(item.usStockCost)}" />
        </label>
        <label>
          備註
          <textarea data-history-field="note" rows="3" placeholder="記錄當天事件">${escapeHTML(item.note || "")}</textarea>
        </label>
        <div class="history-actions">
          <button type="button" data-history-action="save" data-history-date="${escapeHTML(item.date)}">鎖定</button>
          <button type="button" data-history-action="cancel" data-history-date="${escapeHTML(item.date)}">取消</button>
          <button type="button" data-history-action="delete" data-history-date="${escapeHTML(item.date)}">刪除</button>
        </div>
      `;
    } else {
      const noteId = `history-note-${escapeHTML(item.date)}`;
      const extremaBadges = getHistoryExtremaBadges(historyExtrema, item.date);
      const totalExtremaClasses = getHistoryExtremaClasses(historyExtrema, "total", item.date);
      const cashExtremaClasses = getHistoryExtremaClasses(historyExtrema, "cash", item.date);
      const stockExtremaClasses = getHistoryExtremaClasses(historyExtrema, "stockTotal", item.date);
      const fundExtremaClasses = getHistoryExtremaClasses(historyExtrema, "fundTotal", item.date);
      const cryptoExtremaClasses = getHistoryExtremaClasses(historyExtrema, "cryptoTotal", item.date);
      const usStockExtremaClasses = getHistoryExtremaClasses(historyExtrema, "usStockTotal", item.date);
      const previousItem = index > 0 ? historyItems[index - 1] : null;
      const dailyChange = (currentValue, previousValue) => {
        if (!previousItem) return "";
        const change = toNumber(currentValue) - toNumber(previousValue);
        if (change === 0) return "";
        const previousBase = Math.abs(toNumber(previousValue));
        const percent = previousBase ? (change / previousBase) * 100 : 0;
        const roundedThousands = Math.round(Math.abs(change) / 1000);
        const shortChange = `${change >= 0 ? "+" : "-"}$${formatNumber(roundedThousands)}K`;
        const largeChangeClass = Math.abs(percent) > 15 ? " is-large-change" : "";
        return `<small class="history-daily-pnl history-inline-daily${largeChangeClass}">${shortChange} ${percent >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${formatNumber(percent, 2)}%</small>`;
      };
      node.innerHTML = `
        <div class="history-card-head">
          <div class="history-date-line">
            <span>${escapeHTML(item.date)}</span>
            ${extremaBadges}
          </div>
          <div class="history-card-tools">
            ${item.note ? `
              <button class="icon-btn note-toggle" type="button" data-history-action="toggle-note" data-history-date="${escapeHTML(item.date)}" aria-label="查看 ${escapeHTML(item.date)} 重要事件" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="${noteId}" title="重要事件">
                ${starIcon()}
              </button>
            ` : ""}
            <button class="icon-btn" type="button" data-history-action="unlock" data-history-date="${escapeHTML(item.date)}" aria-label="解鎖 ${escapeHTML(item.date)} 歷史紀錄" title="解鎖">
              ${keyIcon()}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="history-total-line ${totalExtremaClasses}">
          <strong>${money(item.total)}</strong>
          ${dailyChange(item.total, previousItem?.total)}
        </div>
        <em class="history-value-row ${cashExtremaClasses}"><span>現金 ${money(item.cash || 0)}</span></em>
        <em class="history-value-row ${stockExtremaClasses}"><span>台股 ${money(item.stockTotal || 0)}</span>${dailyChange(item.stockTotal, previousItem?.stockTotal)}</em>
        <em class="history-value-row ${fundExtremaClasses}"><span>基金 ${money(item.fundTotal || 0)}</span>${dailyChange(item.fundTotal, previousItem?.fundTotal)}</em>
        <em class="history-value-row ${cryptoExtremaClasses}"><span>加密 ${money(item.cryptoTotal || 0)}</span>${dailyChange(item.cryptoTotal, previousItem?.cryptoTotal)}</em>
        <em class="history-value-row ${usStockExtremaClasses}"><span>美股 ${money(item.usStockTotal || 0)}</span>${dailyChange(item.usStockTotal, previousItem?.usStockTotal)}</em>
        ${item.note ? `<p class="history-note" id="${noteId}" data-history-note hidden>${escapeHTML(item.note)}</p>` : ""}
      `;
    }

    const month = String(item.date || "").slice(0, 7) || "未分類";
    monthLists.get(month)?.append(node);
  }
}

function drawCharts() {
  drawAssetCharts();
  drawPieChart();
  drawCategoryPieChart();
  drawCryptoPieChart();
  drawUsStockPieChart();
}

function drawAssetCharts() {
  for (const canvas of els.assetCharts) {
    drawAssetChart(canvas, canvas.dataset.assetChart || "combined");
  }
}

function drawAssetChart(canvas, mode = "combined") {
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  canvas.width = Math.max(320, Math.floor(rect.width * ratio));
  canvas.height = Math.floor(340 * ratio);
  context.scale(ratio, ratio);

  const width = rect.width;
  const height = 340;
  const compactChart = width < 720;
  context.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

  const padding = compactChart
    ? { top: 54, right: 18, bottom: 50, left: 54 }
    : { top: 54, right: 76, bottom: 50, left: 76 };
  const chartWidth = width - padding.left - padding.right;
  const chartHeight = height - padding.top - padding.bottom;
  const history = getChartHistoryWithLivePoint();

  if (history.length === 0) {
    updateAssetChartHoverData(canvas, mode, [], null);
    context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
    context.font = "14px sans-serif";
    context.textAlign = "center";
    context.fillText("尚未有每日結帳紀錄", width / 2, height / 2);
    return;
  }

  const values = history.map((item) => getAssetChartValue(item, mode));
  const min = 0;
  const gridStep = getAssetChartGridStep(mode);
  const max = getAssetChartMax(values, mode, gridStep);
  const range = max - min || Math.max(max, 1);

  const points = history.map((item, index) => {
    const x = padding.left + (history.length === 1 ? chartWidth / 2 : (index / (history.length - 1)) * chartWidth);
    const stockTotal = toNumber(item.stockTotal);
    const fundTotal = toNumber(item.fundTotal);
    const cryptoTotal = toNumber(item.cryptoTotal);
    const cash = getRecordedCash(item);
    const value = getAssetChartValue(item, mode);
    const valueY = padding.top + chartHeight - ((value - min) / range) * chartHeight;
    const fundY = padding.top + chartHeight - ((fundTotal - min) / range) * chartHeight;
    const cryptoY = padding.top + chartHeight - ((fundTotal + cryptoTotal - min) / range) * chartHeight;
    const stockY = padding.top + chartHeight - ((fundTotal + cryptoTotal + stockTotal - min) / range) * chartHeight;
    const totalY = padding.top + chartHeight - ((cryptoTotal + stockTotal + fundTotal + cash - min) / range) * chartHeight;
    return { x, valueY, stockY, fundY, cryptoY, totalY, item, stockTotal, fundTotal, cryptoTotal, cash, value };
  });
  updateAssetChartHoverData(canvas, mode, points, {
    left: padding.left,
    right: padding.left + chartWidth,
    top: padding.top,
    bottom: padding.top + chartHeight,
  });

  if (mode === "combined") {
    if (points.length === 1) {
      drawSingleStackedColumn(context, points[0], padding, chartWidth, chartHeight);
    } else {
      fillStackedArea(context, points, padding.top + chartHeight, "fundY", assetChartColors.fundFill);
      fillStackedBand(context, points, "cryptoY", "fundY", assetChartColors.cryptoFill);
      fillStackedBand(context, points, "stockY", "cryptoY", assetChartColors.stockFill);
      fillStackedBand(context, points, "totalY", "stockY", assetChartColors.cashFill);
    }
  } else {
    fillStackedArea(context, points, padding.top + chartHeight, "valueY", getSingleAssetChartColor(mode, "fill"));
  }

  drawGrid(context, padding, chartWidth, chartHeight, max, gridStep, !compactChart);

  if (mode === "combined") {
    drawAssetLine(context, points, "fundY", assetChartColors.fundLine, 4.5);
    drawAssetLine(context, points, "cryptoY", assetChartColors.cryptoLine, 4.5, (point) => point.cryptoTotal > 0);
    drawAssetLine(context, points, "stockY", assetChartColors.stockLine, 4);
    drawAssetLine(context, points, "totalY", assetChartColors.cashLine, 4.5, (point) => point.cash > 0);
  } else {
    drawAssetLine(context, points, "valueY", getSingleAssetChartColor(mode, "line"), 4.5);
  }

  context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
  context.font = compactChart ? "10px sans-serif" : "12px sans-serif";
  context.textAlign = "center";
  context.textBaseline = "top";
  points.forEach((point, index) => {
    if (!shouldDrawChartDateLabel(index, points.length, compactChart, chartWidth)) return;
    const previousDate = points[index - 1]?.item.date || "";
    const label = formatChartDateLabel(point.item.date, index, points[0].item.date, previousDate);
    context.fillText(label, point.x, padding.top + chartHeight + 22);
  });

  drawAssetLegend(context, padding.left, 24, mode);
}

function setupAssetChartHover(canvas) {
  const slide = canvas.closest(".asset-chart-slide");
  if (!slide) return;

  const tooltip = document.createElement("div");
  tooltip.className = "asset-chart-tooltip";
  tooltip.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  slide.append(tooltip);
  assetChartInteractions.set(canvas, {
    tooltip,
    mode: canvas.dataset.assetChart || "combined",
    points: [],
    bounds: null,
  });

  canvas.addEventListener("mousemove", (event) => {
    if (window.innerWidth <= 640) {
      hideAssetChartTooltip(canvas);
      return;
    }

    const interaction = assetChartInteractions.get(canvas);
    if (!interaction?.points.length) return;
    const canvasRect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    const pointerX = (event.clientX - canvasRect.left) * (canvas.width / ratio / canvasRect.width);
    const pointerY = (event.clientY - canvasRect.top) * (canvas.height / ratio / canvasRect.height);
    const nearest = findHoveredAssetChartPoint(
      interaction.points,
      pointerX,
      pointerY,
      interaction.bounds,
    );
    if (!nearest) {
      hideAssetChartTooltip(canvas);
      return;
    }

    showAssetChartTooltip(canvas, nearest.point, event.clientX, event.clientY);
  });
  canvas.addEventListener("mouseleave", () => hideAssetChartTooltip(canvas));
}

function updateAssetChartHoverData(canvas, mode, points, bounds) {
  const interaction = assetChartInteractions.get(canvas);
  if (!interaction) return;
  interaction.mode = mode;
  interaction.points = points;
  interaction.bounds = bounds;
  hideAssetChartTooltip(canvas);
}

function findHoveredAssetChartPoint(points, pointerX, pointerY, bounds) {
  if (!points.length || !bounds) return null;
  if (
    pointerX < bounds.left
    || pointerX > bounds.right
    || pointerY < bounds.top
    || pointerY > bounds.bottom
  ) return null;

  return points.reduce((nearest, point) => {
    const distance = Math.abs(pointerX - point.x);
    return !nearest || distance < nearest.distance ? { point, distance } : nearest;
  }, null);
}

function getAssetChartTooltipRows(point, mode) {
  if (mode === "combined") {
    return [
      { label: "合併市值", value: point.value, color: "#17211c" },
      { label: "現金", value: point.cash, color: assetChartColors.cashLine },
      { label: "台灣股市", value: point.stockTotal, color: assetChartColors.stockLine },
      { label: "加密", value: point.cryptoTotal, color: assetChartColors.cryptoLine },
      { label: "基金", value: point.fundTotal, color: assetChartColors.fundLine },
    ];
  }

  const singleRows = {
    cash: { label: "現金", color: assetChartColors.cashLine },
    stock: { label: "台灣股市", color: assetChartColors.stockLine },
    crypto: { label: "加密", color: assetChartColors.cryptoLine },
    fund: { label: "基金", color: assetChartColors.fundLine },
    usStock: { label: "美國股市", color: assetChartColors.usStockLine },
  };
  const row = singleRows[mode] || singleRows.stock;
  return [{ ...row, value: point.value }];
}

function showAssetChartTooltip(canvas, point, pointerX, pointerY) {
  const interaction = assetChartInteractions.get(canvas);
  if (!interaction) return;

  const rows = getAssetChartTooltipRows(point, interaction.mode)
    .filter((row) => toNumber(row.value) !== 0);
  if (!rows.length) {
    hideAssetChartTooltip(canvas);
    return;
  }

  interaction.tooltip.innerHTML = `
    <strong>${escapeHTML(point.item.date || "")}</strong>
    ${rows.map((row) => `
      <span class="asset-chart-tooltip-row">
        <span>${row.label}</span>
        <b>${money(row.value)}</b>
      </span>
    `).join("")}
  `;
  interaction.tooltip.classList.add("is-visible");
  interaction.tooltip.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "false");

  const tooltipWidth = interaction.tooltip.offsetWidth;
  const tooltipHeight = interaction.tooltip.offsetHeight;
  const left = Math.min(window.innerWidth - tooltipWidth - 8, Math.max(8, pointerX - tooltipWidth / 2));
  const top = Math.max(8, pointerY - tooltipHeight - 14);
  interaction.tooltip.style.left = `${left}px`;
  interaction.tooltip.style.top = `${top}px`;
}

function hideAssetChartTooltip(canvas) {
  const tooltip = assetChartInteractions.get(canvas)?.tooltip;
  if (!tooltip) return;
  tooltip.classList.remove("is-visible");
  tooltip.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
}

function getChartHistoryWithLivePoint() {
  const history = state.history.map((item) => ({ ...item }));
  const totals = getPortfolioTotals();
  if (!totals.value) return history;

  const date = todayISO();
  const livePoint = {
    date,
    total: totals.value,
    stockTotal: totals.stockTotal,
    usStockTotal: totals.usStockTotal,
    fundTotal: totals.fundTotal,
    cryptoTotal: totals.cryptoTotal,
    cash: totals.cash,
    cashRecorded: true,
  };
  const existingIndex = history.findIndex((item) => item.date === date);
  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    history[existingIndex] = { ...history[existingIndex], ...livePoint };
  } else {
    history.push(livePoint);
  }
  return history.sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
}

function getAssetChartValue(item, mode) {
  const stockTotal = toNumber(item.stockTotal);
  const usStockTotal = toNumber(item.usStockTotal);
  const fundTotal = toNumber(item.fundTotal);
  const cryptoTotal = toNumber(item.cryptoTotal);
  const cash = getRecordedCash(item);
  if (mode === "stock") return stockTotal;
  if (mode === "usStock") return usStockTotal;
  if (mode === "fund") return fundTotal;
  if (mode === "cash") return cash;
  if (mode === "crypto") return cryptoTotal;
  return stockTotal + fundTotal + cryptoTotal + cash;
}

function hasRecordedCash(item) {
  return Boolean(item.cashRecorded) || (String(item.date || "") >= "2026-07-15" && toNumber(item.cash) > 0);
}

function getRecordedCash(item) {
  return hasRecordedCash(item) ? toNumber(item.cash) : 0;
}

function getAssetChartGridStep(mode) {
  if (mode === "crypto") return 100000;
  return mode === "fund" || mode === "usStock" ? 100000 : 500000;
}

function getAssetChartMax(values, mode, gridStep) {
  const highest = Math.max(...values);
  if (mode === "fund" || mode === "crypto") {
    return Math.max(gridStep, Math.floor(highest / gridStep) * gridStep + 200000);
  }
  return Math.ceil(highest / gridStep) * gridStep || gridStep;
}

function getSingleAssetChartColor(mode, type) {
  if (mode === "fund") return type === "line" ? assetChartColors.fundLine : assetChartColors.fundFill;
  if (mode === "cash") return type === "line" ? assetChartColors.cashLine : assetChartColors.cashFill;
  if (mode === "crypto") return type === "line" ? assetChartColors.cryptoLine : assetChartColors.cryptoFill;
  if (mode === "usStock") return type === "line" ? assetChartColors.usStockLine : assetChartColors.usStockFill;
  return type === "line" ? assetChartColors.stockLine : assetChartColors.stockFill;
}

function shouldDrawChartDateLabel(index, total, compactChart, chartWidth = 320) {
  if (!compactChart) {
    if (total <= 7) return true;
    const desktopLabelIndexes = Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, slot) =>
      Math.round((slot * (total - 1)) / 6)
    );
    return desktopLabelIndexes.includes(index);
  }
  if (total <= 4) return true;
  const mobileLabelIndexes = Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, slot) =>
    Math.round((slot * (total - 1)) / 3)
  );
  return mobileLabelIndexes.includes(index);
}

function formatChartDateLabel(date, index, firstDate, previousDate) {
  const current = String(date || "");
  const [year, month, day] = current.split("-");
  if (!year || !month || !day) return current.replaceAll("-", "");
  if (index === 0) return `${year}${month}${day}`;

  const firstYear = String(firstDate || "").slice(0, 4);
  const previousYear = String(previousDate || "").slice(0, 4);
  if (year !== firstYear && year !== previousYear) return `${year}${month}${day}`;

  return `${month}${day}`;
}

function drawPieChart() {
  const canvas = els.pieChart;
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const width = rect.width || 320;
  const compactPie = width < 720;

  const slices = state.holdings
    .map((holding) => ({
      label: holding.name || holding.symbol,
      symbol: holding.symbol,
      group: getHoldingGroupName(holding),
      value: holding.shares * (holding.currentPrice ?? holding.avgCost),
    }))
    .filter((slice) => slice.value > 0);
  const groupTotals = new Map();
  for (const slice of slices) {
    groupTotals.set(slice.group, (groupTotals.get(slice.group) || 0) + slice.value);
  }
  const stockGroupNames = [...groupTotals.keys()].sort((a, b) =>
    groupTotals.get(b) - groupTotals.get(a) || chineseNameSorter.compare(a, b)
  );
  const groupRanks = new Map(stockGroupNames.map((group, index) => [group, index]));
  slices.sort((a, b) =>
    groupRanks.get(a.group) - groupRanks.get(b.group)
      || b.value - a.value
      || chineseNameSorter.compare(a.label, b.label)
  );
  if (toNumber(state.cash) > 0) {
    slices.unshift({
      label: "Cash",
      symbol: "Cash",
      group: "Cash",
      value: toNumber(state.cash),
      isCash: true,
    });
  }
  const groupNames = toNumber(state.cash) > 0 ? ["Cash", ...stockGroupNames] : stockGroupNames;
  const groupColorFamilies = assignPieColorFamilies(slices);
  const groupCounts = {};
  const coloredSlices = slices
    .map((slice) => ({
      ...slice,
      color: getGroupedPieColor(slice.group, groupColorFamilies, groupCounts),
    }));
  slices.splice(0, slices.length, ...coloredSlices);
  const total = slices.reduce((sum, slice) => sum + slice.value, 0);
  const summaryRows = buildPieGroupSummary(slices, groupNames, groupColorFamilies, total);
  const chartHeight = compactPie ? 640 : 880;
  const summaryHeight = getPieGroupSummaryHeight(summaryRows, width, compactPie);
