  const height = chartHeight + summaryHeight;
  canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;
  canvas.width = Math.max(320, Math.floor(width * ratio));
  canvas.height = Math.floor(height * ratio);
  context.scale(ratio, ratio);
  context.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

  if (!total) {
    context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
    context.font = "14px sans-serif";
    context.textAlign = "center";
    context.fillText("尚未有台灣股市市值", width / 2, height / 2);
    return;
  }

  const labelRoom = compactPie ? 104 : 180;
  const radius = compactPie ? Math.min((width - labelRoom * 2) / 2, 76) : Math.min((width - labelRoom * 2) / 2, chartHeight * 0.34, 210);
  const centerX = width / 2;
  const centerY = chartHeight / 2;
  const cashAngle = slices[0]?.isCash ? (slices[0].value / total) * Math.PI * 2 : 0;
  let start = cashAngle ? -Math.PI / 2 - cashAngle : -Math.PI / 2;

  for (const slice of slices) {
    const angle = (slice.value / total) * Math.PI * 2;
    slice.start = start;
    slice.end = start + angle;
    context.beginPath();
    context.moveTo(centerX, centerY);
    context.arc(centerX, centerY, radius, start, start + angle);
    context.closePath();
    context.fillStyle = slice.color;
    context.fill();
    context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.32)";
    context.lineWidth = 0.8;
    context.stroke();
    start += angle;
  }

  const outsideLabels = [];
  const insideLabelMids = slices
    .filter((slice) => (slice.value / total) * 100 >= (compactPie ? 16 : 12))
    .map((slice) => (slice.start + slice.end) / 2);
  for (const slice of slices) {
    const percent = (slice.value / total) * 100;
    const mid = (slice.start + slice.end) / 2;
    const lines = [slice.label, `${formatNumber(percent)}%`, money(slice.value)];
    if (percent >= (compactPie ? 16 : 12)) {
      const labelRadius = radius * getInsidePieLabelRadiusFactor(
        mid,
        slice.end - slice.start,
        insideLabelMids,
        compactPie,
        0.58,
      );
      const x = centerX + Math.cos(mid) * labelRadius;
      const y = centerY + Math.sin(mid) * labelRadius;
      context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
      context.font = compactPie ? "700 8.5px sans-serif" : "700 11.5px sans-serif";
      context.textAlign = "center";
      context.textBaseline = "middle";
      drawPieLabelLines(context, lines, x, y, compactPie ? 10 : 14);
    } else {
      outsideLabels.push({
        lines,
        color: slice.color,
        side: Math.cos(mid) >= 0 ? "right" : "left",
        anchorX: centerX + Math.cos(mid) * radius,
        anchorY: centerY + Math.sin(mid) * radius,
        targetY: centerY + Math.sin(mid) * (radius + 30),
        compact: compactPie,
      });
    }
  }

  if (compactPie) {
    drawMobileOutsidePieLabels(context, outsideLabels, centerX, centerY, radius, chartHeight, width);
  } else {
    drawOutsidePieLabels(context, outsideLabels, centerX, radius, chartHeight);
  }
  drawPieGroupSummary(context, summaryRows, width, chartHeight, summaryHeight, compactPie);
}

function drawCategoryPieChart() {
  const canvas = els.categoryPieChart;
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const width = rect.width || 320;
  const compactPie = width < 720;
  const totals = getPortfolioTotals();
  const categorySlices = [
    { label: "Cash", group: "Cash", value: totals.cash, isCash: true },
    { label: "台灣股市", group: "台灣股市", value: totals.stockTotal },
    { label: "美國股市", group: "美國股市", value: totals.usStockTotal },
    { label: "基金", group: "基金", value: totals.fundTotal },
    { label: "加密合約", group: "加密合約", value: totals.cryptoTotal },
  ].filter((slice) => slice.value > 0);
  const cashSlice = categorySlices.find((slice) => slice.isCash);
  const slices = categorySlices
    .filter((slice) => !slice.isCash)
    .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value || chineseNameSorter.compare(a.label, b.label));
  if (cashSlice) slices.unshift(cashSlice);
  const groupColorFamilies = assignPieColorFamilies(slices);
  const groupCounts = {};
  for (const slice of slices) {
    slice.color = getGroupedPieColor(slice.group, groupColorFamilies, groupCounts);
  }
  const total = slices.reduce((sum, slice) => sum + slice.value, 0);
  const summaryRows = slices.map((slice) => ({
    group: slice.label,
    value: slice.value,
    percent: total ? (slice.value / total) * 100 : 0,
    color: getPieGroupBaseColor(slice.group, groupColorFamilies),
  }));
  const chartHeight = compactPie ? Math.max(320, width) : Math.max(620, Math.min(760, width * 0.62));
  const summaryHeight = getPieFlowSummaryHeight(context, summaryRows, width, compactPie);
  const height = chartHeight + summaryHeight;

  canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;
  canvas.width = Math.max(320, Math.floor(width * ratio));
  canvas.height = Math.floor(height * ratio);
  context.scale(ratio, ratio);
  context.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

  if (!total) {
    context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
    context.font = "14px sans-serif";
    context.textAlign = "center";
    context.fillText("尚未有資產資料", width / 2, height / 2);
    return;
  }

  const labelRoom = compactPie ? 12 : 180;
  const radius = compactPie ? Math.min(width / 2 - labelRoom, 180) : Math.min((width - labelRoom * 2) / 2, chartHeight * 0.34, 210);
  const centerX = width / 2;
  const centerY = chartHeight / 2;
  const cashAngle = slices[0]?.isCash ? (slices[0].value / total) * Math.PI * 2 : 0;
  let start = cashAngle ? -Math.PI / 2 - cashAngle : -Math.PI / 2;

  for (const slice of slices) {
    const angle = (slice.value / total) * Math.PI * 2;
    slice.start = start;
    slice.end = start + angle;
    context.beginPath();
    context.moveTo(centerX, centerY);
    context.arc(centerX, centerY, radius, start, start + angle);
    context.closePath();
    context.fillStyle = slice.color;
    context.fill();
    context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.32)";
    context.lineWidth = 0.8;
    context.stroke();
    start += angle;
  }

  const insideLabels = [];
  const outsideLabels = [];
  const insideLabelMids = slices
    .filter((slice) => compactPie || (slice.value / total) * 100 >= 12)
    .map((slice) => (slice.start + slice.end) / 2);
  for (const slice of slices) {
    const percent = (slice.value / total) * 100;
    const mid = (slice.start + slice.end) / 2;
    const lines = [slice.label, `${formatNumber(percent)}%`, money(slice.value)];
    if (compactPie) {
      insideLabels.push({ lines, mid, angle: slice.end - slice.start, percent });
    } else if (percent >= 12) {
      const labelRadius = radius * getInsidePieLabelRadiusFactor(
        mid,
        slice.end - slice.start,
        insideLabelMids,
        compactPie,
        0.58,
      );
      context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
      context.font = compactPie ? "700 8px sans-serif" : "700 11.5px sans-serif";
      context.textAlign = "center";
      context.textBaseline = "middle";
      drawPieLabelLines(context, lines, centerX + Math.cos(mid) * labelRadius, centerY + Math.sin(mid) * labelRadius, compactPie ? 9 : 14);
    } else {
      outsideLabels.push({
        lines,
        color: slice.color,
        side: Math.cos(mid) >= 0 ? "right" : "left",
        anchorX: centerX + Math.cos(mid) * radius,
        anchorY: centerY + Math.sin(mid) * radius,
        targetY: centerY + Math.sin(mid) * (radius + 30),
      });
    }
  }

  if (compactPie) {
    drawSeparatedInsidePieLabels(context, insideLabels, centerX, centerY, radius, "700 8px sans-serif", 9);
    drawMobileOutsidePieLabels(context, outsideLabels, centerX, centerY, radius, chartHeight, width);
  } else {
    drawOutsidePieLabels(context, outsideLabels, centerX, radius, chartHeight);
  }
  drawPieGroupSummary(context, summaryRows, width, chartHeight, summaryHeight, compactPie, "資產類別小計", true);
}

function drawCryptoPieChart() {
  const canvas = els.cryptoPieChart;
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const width = rect.width || 320;
  const compactPie = width < 720;
  const slices = state.cryptoContracts
    .map((contract) => {
      const position = calculateCryptoPosition(contract);
      const sideLabel = contract.side === "short" ? "做空" : "做多";
      const contractLabel = `${contract.name || "未命名合約"} X${formatNumber(contract.leverage)} ${sideLabel}`;
      return {
        label: contractLabel,
        group: contractLabel,
        value: position.currentValueTwd,
      };
    })
    .filter((slice) => slice.value > 0);

  const groupTotals = new Map();
  for (const slice of slices) {
    groupTotals.set(slice.group, (groupTotals.get(slice.group) || 0) + slice.value);
  }
  const groupNames = [...groupTotals.keys()].sort((a, b) =>
    groupTotals.get(b) - groupTotals.get(a) || chineseNameSorter.compare(a, b)
  );
  const groupRanks = new Map(groupNames.map((group, index) => [group, index]));
  slices.sort((a, b) =>
    groupRanks.get(a.group) - groupRanks.get(b.group)
      || b.value - a.value
      || chineseNameSorter.compare(a.label, b.label)
  );

  const groupColorFamilies = assignPieColorFamilies(slices);
  const groupCounts = {};
  for (const slice of slices) {
    slice.color = getGroupedPieColor(slice.group, groupColorFamilies, groupCounts);
  }

  const total = slices.reduce((sum, slice) => sum + slice.value, 0);
  const summaryRows = buildPieGroupSummary(slices, groupNames, groupColorFamilies, total);
  const chartHeight = compactPie ? Math.max(320, width) : Math.max(620, Math.min(760, width * 0.62));
  const summaryHeight = getPieFlowSummaryHeight(context, summaryRows, width, compactPie);
  const height = chartHeight + summaryHeight;

  canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;
  canvas.width = Math.max(320, Math.floor(width * ratio));
  canvas.height = Math.floor(height * ratio);
  context.scale(ratio, ratio);
  context.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);

  if (!total) {
    context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
    context.font = "14px sans-serif";
    context.textAlign = "center";
    context.fillText("尚未有加密合約資料", width / 2, height / 2);
    return;
  }

  const labelRoom = compactPie ? 12 : 180;
  const radius = compactPie ? Math.min(width / 2 - labelRoom, 180) : Math.min((width - labelRoom * 2) / 2, chartHeight * 0.34, 210);
  const centerX = width / 2;
  const centerY = chartHeight / 2;
  let start = -Math.PI / 2;

  for (const slice of slices) {
    const angle = (slice.value / total) * Math.PI * 2;
    slice.start = start;
    slice.end = start + angle;
    context.beginPath();
    context.moveTo(centerX, centerY);
    context.arc(centerX, centerY, radius, start, start + angle);
    context.closePath();
    context.fillStyle = slice.color;
    context.fill();
    context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.32)";
    context.lineWidth = 0.8;
    context.stroke();
    start += angle;
  }

  const outsideLabels = [];
  const insideLabelMids = slices
    .filter((slice) => compactPie || (slice.value / total) * 100 >= 12)
    .map((slice) => (slice.start + slice.end) / 2);
  for (const slice of slices) {
    const percent = (slice.value / total) * 100;
    const mid = (slice.start + slice.end) / 2;
    const lines = [slice.label, `${formatNumber(percent)}%`, money(slice.value)];
    if (compactPie || percent >= 12) {
      const baseFactor = compactPie ? 0.62 : 0.58;
      const labelRadius = radius * getInsidePieLabelRadiusFactor(
        mid,
        slice.end - slice.start,
        insideLabelMids,
        compactPie,
        baseFactor,
      );
      context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
      context.font = compactPie ? "700 8px sans-serif" : "700 11.5px sans-serif";
      context.textAlign = "center";
      context.textBaseline = "middle";
      drawPieLabelLines(context, lines, centerX + Math.cos(mid) * labelRadius, centerY + Math.sin(mid) * labelRadius, compactPie ? 9 : 14);
    } else {
      outsideLabels.push({
        lines,
        color: slice.color,
        side: Math.cos(mid) >= 0 ? "right" : "left",
        anchorX: centerX + Math.cos(mid) * radius,
        anchorY: centerY + Math.sin(mid) * radius,
        targetY: centerY + Math.sin(mid) * (radius + 30),
      });
    }
  }

  if (compactPie) {
    drawMobileOutsidePieLabels(context, outsideLabels, centerX, centerY, radius, chartHeight, width);
  } else {
    drawOutsidePieLabels(context, outsideLabels, centerX, radius, chartHeight);
  }
  drawPieGroupSummary(context, summaryRows, width, chartHeight, summaryHeight, compactPie, "加密合約小計", true);
}

function drawUsStockPieChart() {
  const canvas = els.usStockPieChart;
  const context = canvas.getContext("2d");
  const ratio = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const width = rect.width || 320;
  const compactPie = width < 720;
  const slices = state.usHoldings
    .map((holding) => ({
      label: holding.symbol,
      group: holding.symbol,
      value: holding.shares * (holding.currentPrice ?? holding.avgCost) * (state.usdTwdRate || 0),
    }))
    .filter((slice) => slice.value > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => b.value - a.value || chineseNameSorter.compare(a.label, b.label));
  const groupColorFamilies = assignPieColorFamilies(slices);
  const groupCounts = {};
  for (const slice of slices) slice.color = getGroupedPieColor(slice.group, groupColorFamilies, groupCounts);
  const total = slices.reduce((sum, slice) => sum + slice.value, 0);
  const groupNames = [...new Set(slices.map((slice) => slice.group))];
  const summaryRows = buildPieGroupSummary(slices, groupNames, groupColorFamilies, total);
  const chartHeight = compactPie ? Math.max(320, width) : Math.max(620, Math.min(760, width * 0.62));
  const summaryHeight = getPieGroupSummaryHeight(summaryRows, width, compactPie);
  const height = chartHeight + summaryHeight;

  canvas.style.height = `${height}px`;
  canvas.width = Math.max(320, Math.floor(width * ratio));
  canvas.height = Math.floor(height * ratio);
  context.scale(ratio, ratio);
  context.clearRect(0, 0, width, height);
  if (!total) {
    context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
    context.font = "14px sans-serif";
    context.textAlign = "center";
    context.fillText("尚未有美股市值", width / 2, height / 2);
    return;
  }

  const labelRoom = compactPie ? 12 : 180;
  const radius = compactPie ? Math.min(width / 2 - labelRoom, 180) : Math.min((width - labelRoom * 2) / 2, chartHeight * 0.34, 210);
  const centerX = width / 2;
  const centerY = chartHeight / 2;
  let start = -Math.PI / 2;
  for (const slice of slices) {
    const angle = (slice.value / total) * Math.PI * 2;
    slice.start = start;
    slice.end = start + angle;
    context.beginPath();
    context.moveTo(centerX, centerY);
    context.arc(centerX, centerY, radius, start, start + angle);
    context.closePath();
    context.fillStyle = slice.color;
    context.fill();
    context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.32)";
    context.lineWidth = 0.8;
    context.stroke();
    start += angle;
  }

  const insideLabels = [];
  const outsideLabels = [];
  const insideLabelMids = slices
    .filter((slice) => compactPie || (slice.value / total) * 100 >= 12)
    .map((slice) => (slice.start + slice.end) / 2);
  for (const slice of slices) {
    const percent = (slice.value / total) * 100;
    const mid = (slice.start + slice.end) / 2;
    const lines = [slice.label, `${formatNumber(percent)}%`, money(slice.value)];
    if (compactPie) {
      insideLabels.push({ lines, mid, angle: slice.end - slice.start, percent });
    } else if (percent >= 12) {
      const labelRadius = radius * getInsidePieLabelRadiusFactor(
        mid,
        slice.end - slice.start,
        insideLabelMids,
        compactPie,
        0.58,
      );
      context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
      context.font = compactPie ? "700 8px sans-serif" : "700 11.5px sans-serif";
      context.textAlign = "center";
      context.textBaseline = "middle";
      drawPieLabelLines(context, lines, centerX + Math.cos(mid) * labelRadius, centerY + Math.sin(mid) * labelRadius, compactPie ? 9 : 14);
    } else {
      outsideLabels.push({
        lines,
        color: slice.color,
        side: Math.cos(mid) >= 0 ? "right" : "left",
        anchorX: centerX + Math.cos(mid) * radius,
        anchorY: centerY + Math.sin(mid) * radius,
        targetY: centerY + Math.sin(mid) * (radius + 30),
      });
    }
  }
  if (compactPie) {
    drawSeparatedInsidePieLabels(context, insideLabels, centerX, centerY, radius, "700 8px sans-serif", 9);
  }
  if (compactPie) drawMobileOutsidePieLabels(context, outsideLabels, centerX, centerY, radius, chartHeight, width);
  else drawOutsidePieLabels(context, outsideLabels, centerX, radius, chartHeight);
  drawPieGroupSummary(context, summaryRows, width, chartHeight, summaryHeight, compactPie, "美國股市小計");
}

function getHoldingGroupName(holding) {
  const rawName = String(holding.name || holding.symbol || "").trim();
  const compactName = stripBrokerNames(rawName.replace(/\s+/g, ""));
  const starred = compactName.match(/^([\u4e00-\u9fff]+)\*/);
  if (starred?.[1]) return `${starred[1]}*`;
  const matched = compactName.match(/^([\u4e00-\u9fff]+?)(?:[A-Z]*\d[A-Z0-9]*.*)$/i);
  if (matched?.[1]) return matched[1];
  return rawName || "未命名";
}

function compareHoldingsByDisplayName(a, b) {
  const groupCompare = chineseNameSorter.compare(getHoldingGroupName(a), getHoldingGroupName(b));
  if (groupCompare) return groupCompare;
  return compareStockCodes(getHoldingTailCode(a), getHoldingTailCode(b))
    || compareStockCodes(a.symbol, b.symbol);
}

function getHoldingTailCode(holding) {
  return getNameTailCode(holding.name || holding.symbol, holding.symbol);
}

function getNameTailCode(name, fallback = "") {
  const compactName = stripBrokerNames(String(name || "").replace(/\s+/g, "").toUpperCase());
  const matched = compactName.match(/([0-9A-Z]{4})$/);
  if (matched?.[1]) return matched[1];
  return String(fallback || "").toUpperCase().slice(-4);
}

function stripBrokerNames(name) {
  return name.replace(/(?:中國信託|華南永昌|第一金|元大|凱基|群益|富邦|國泰|永豐|統一|兆豐|台新|元富|中信|玉山|日盛|康和|宏遠)/g, "");
}

function assignPieColorFamilies(slices) {
  const visualGroups = [];
  for (const slice of slices) {
    if (visualGroups[visualGroups.length - 1] !== slice.group) visualGroups.push(slice.group);
  }

  const assignments = new Map();
  const usage = Array(pieColorFamilies.length).fill(0);

  visualGroups.forEach((group, index) => {
    const previousFamily = index > 0 ? assignments.get(visualGroups[index - 1]) : undefined;
    const firstFamily = assignments.get(visualGroups[0]);
    const forbidden = new Set();
    if (previousFamily !== undefined) forbidden.add(previousFamily);
    if (index === visualGroups.length - 1 && visualGroups.length > 1 && firstFamily !== undefined) {
      forbidden.add(firstFamily);
    }

    let selected = 0;
    let selectedUsage = Infinity;
    for (let familyIndex = 0; familyIndex < pieColorFamilies.length; familyIndex += 1) {
      if (forbidden.has(familyIndex)) continue;
      if (usage[familyIndex] < selectedUsage) {
        selected = familyIndex;
        selectedUsage = usage[familyIndex];
      }
    }

    assignments.set(group, selected);
    usage[selected] += 1;
  });

  return assignments;
}

function getGroupedPieColor(group, groupColorFamilies, groupCounts) {
  const groupIndex = groupColorFamilies.get(group) ?? 0;
  const family = pieColorFamilies[groupIndex % pieColorFamilies.length];
  const shadeIndex = groupCounts[group] || 0;
  groupCounts[group] = shadeIndex + 1;
  return family[shadeIndex % family.length];
}

function assignPieGroupOrder(groupNames) {
  const preferred = ["中砂", "同欣電", "佳必琪", "南亞科", "致茂", "智邦", "Cash"];
  return [...groupNames].sort((a, b) => {
    const preferredA = preferred.indexOf(a);
    const preferredB = preferred.indexOf(b);
    const rankA = preferredA >= 0 ? preferredA : preferred.length + groupNames.indexOf(a);
    const rankB = preferredB >= 0 ? preferredB : preferred.length + groupNames.indexOf(b);
    return rankA - rankB;
  });
}

function buildPieGroupSummary(slices, groupNames, groupColorFamilies, total) {
  const summary = new Map();
  for (const slice of slices) {
    const current = summary.get(slice.group) || 0;
    summary.set(slice.group, current + slice.value);
  }
  return groupNames
    .filter((group) => summary.has(group))
    .map((group) => ({
      group,
      value: summary.get(group),
      percent: total ? (summary.get(group) / total) * 100 : 0,
      color: getPieGroupBaseColor(group, groupColorFamilies),
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.group === "Cash") return -1;
      if (b.group === "Cash") return 1;
      return b.value - a.value || chineseNameSorter.compare(a.group, b.group);
    });
}

function getPieGroupBaseColor(group, groupColorFamilies) {
  const groupIndex = groupColorFamilies.get(group) ?? 0;
  const family = pieColorFamilies[groupIndex % pieColorFamilies.length];
  return family[3] ?? family[family.length - 1];
}

function compareStockCodes(a = "", b = "") {
  const left = String(a).toUpperCase();
  const right = String(b).toUpperCase();
  const length = Math.max(left.length, right.length);
  for (let index = 0; index < length; index += 1) {
    if (index >= left.length) return -1;
    if (index >= right.length) return 1;
    const leftRank = stockCodeOrder.indexOf(left[index] || "");
    const rightRank = stockCodeOrder.indexOf(right[index] || "");
    const safeLeftRank = leftRank >= 0 ? leftRank : stockCodeOrder.length + left.charCodeAt(index);
    const safeRightRank = rightRank >= 0 ? rightRank : stockCodeOrder.length + right.charCodeAt(index);
    if (safeLeftRank !== safeRightRank) return safeLeftRank - safeRightRank;
  }
  return 0;
}

function fillStackedArea(context, points, baselineY, key, color) {
  context.fillStyle = color;
  context.beginPath();
  points.forEach((point, index) => {
    if (index === 0) context.moveTo(point.x, baselineY);
    context.lineTo(point.x, point[key]);
  });
  for (let index = points.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
    context.lineTo(points[index].x, baselineY);
  }
  context.closePath();
  context.fill();
}

function fillStackedBand(context, points, topKey, bottomKey, color) {
  context.fillStyle = color;
  context.beginPath();
  points.forEach((point, index) => {
    if (index === 0) context.moveTo(point.x, point[bottomKey]);
    context.lineTo(point.x, point[topKey]);
  });
  for (let index = points.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
    context.lineTo(points[index].x, points[index][bottomKey]);
  }
  context.closePath();
  context.fill();
}

function drawSingleStackedColumn(context, point, padding, chartWidth, chartHeight) {
  const baselineY = padding.top + chartHeight;
  const columnWidth = Math.min(120, Math.max(54, chartWidth * 0.22));
  const x = point.x - columnWidth / 2;

  context.fillStyle = assetChartColors.fundFill;
  context.fillRect(x, point.fundY, columnWidth, baselineY - point.fundY);
  context.fillStyle = assetChartColors.cryptoFill;
  context.fillRect(x, point.cryptoY, columnWidth, point.fundY - point.cryptoY);
  context.fillStyle = assetChartColors.stockFill;
  context.fillRect(x, point.stockY, columnWidth, point.cryptoY - point.stockY);
  context.fillStyle = assetChartColors.cashFill;
  context.fillRect(x, point.totalY, columnWidth, point.stockY - point.totalY);

  context.strokeStyle = assetChartColors.fundLine;
  context.lineWidth = 1.5;
  context.strokeRect(x, point.fundY, columnWidth, baselineY - point.fundY);
  context.strokeStyle = assetChartColors.cryptoLine;
  context.strokeRect(x, point.cryptoY, columnWidth, point.fundY - point.cryptoY);
  context.strokeStyle = assetChartColors.stockLine;
  context.strokeRect(x, point.stockY, columnWidth, point.cryptoY - point.stockY);
  context.strokeStyle = assetChartColors.cashLine;
  context.strokeRect(x, point.totalY, columnWidth, point.stockY - point.totalY);
}

function drawAssetLine(context, points, key, color, pointRadius, shouldDraw = () => true) {
  context.strokeStyle = color;
  context.lineWidth = 2;
  context.beginPath();
  let hasLine = false;
  let isDrawing = false;
  for (const point of points) {
    if (!shouldDraw(point)) {
      isDrawing = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (!isDrawing) {
      context.moveTo(point.x, point[key]);
      isDrawing = true;
    } else {
      context.lineTo(point.x, point[key]);
    }
    hasLine = true;
  }
  if (hasLine) context.stroke();

  for (const point of points) {
    if (!shouldDraw(point)) continue;
    context.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
    context.strokeStyle = color;
    context.lineWidth = 2;
    context.beginPath();
    context.arc(point.x, point[key], pointRadius, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    context.fill();
    context.stroke();
  }
}

function drawAssetLegend(context, x, y, mode = "combined") {
  const items = mode === "stock"
    ? [{ label: "台灣股市", color: assetChartColors.stockFill }]
    : mode === "fund"
      ? [{ label: "基金", color: assetChartColors.fundFill }]
      : mode === "cash"
      ? [{ label: "現金", color: assetChartColors.cashFill }]
      : mode === "crypto"
        ? [{ label: "加密", color: assetChartColors.cryptoFill }]
        : mode === "usStock"
          ? [{ label: "美國股市", color: assetChartColors.usStockFill }]
        : [
            { label: "現金", color: assetChartColors.cashFill },
            { label: "台灣股市", color: assetChartColors.stockFill },
            { label: "加密", color: assetChartColors.cryptoFill },
            { label: "基金", color: assetChartColors.fundFill },
          ];
  context.font = "12px sans-serif";
  context.textAlign = "left";
  context.textBaseline = "middle";
  for (const item of items) {
    context.fillStyle = item.color;
    context.fillRect(x, y - 7, 12, 12);
    context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
    context.fillText(item.label, x + 18, y);
    x += 18 + context.measureText(item.label).width + 18;
  }
}

function drawWrappedLabel(context, text, x, y, maxWidth, lineHeight) {
  const words = text.split(" ");
  const lines = [];
  let current = "";
  for (const word of words) {
    const next = current ? `${current} ${word}` : word;
    if (context.measureText(next).width > maxWidth && current) {
      lines.push(current);
      current = word;
    } else {
      current = next;
    }
  }
  if (current) lines.push(current);
  const offset = ((lines.length - 1) * lineHeight) / 2;
  lines.forEach((line, index) => context.fillText(line, x, y - offset + index * lineHeight));
}

function drawPieLeaderLine(context, label, centerX, centerY, markerX, markerY, compact) {
  const outwardX = label.anchorX - centerX;
  const outwardY = label.anchorY - centerY;
  const directX = markerX - label.anchorX;
  const directY = markerY - label.anchorY;
  const directLengthSquared = directX ** 2 + directY ** 2;
  const closestPoint = directLengthSquared
    ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, -(outwardX * directX + outwardY * directY) / directLengthSquared))
    : 0;
  const radius = Math.hypot(outwardX, outwardY);
  const closestDistance = Math.hypot(
    outwardX + directX * closestPoint,
    outwardY + directY * closestPoint
  );
  const penetration = radius - closestDistance;
  const needsBend = penetration > (compact ? 4 : 6);

  context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.58)";
  context.lineWidth = compact ? 0.9 : 1.05;
  context.lineJoin = "round";
  context.beginPath();
  context.moveTo(label.anchorX, label.anchorY);

  if (needsBend) {
    const length = Math.hypot(outwardX, outwardY) || 1;
    const offset = compact ? 6 : 9;
    context.lineTo(
      label.anchorX + (outwardX / length) * offset,
      label.anchorY + (outwardY / length) * offset
    );
  }

  context.lineTo(markerX, markerY);
  context.stroke();
}

function drawSeparatedInsidePieLabels(context, labels, centerX, centerY, radius, font, lineHeight) {
  const placed = [];
  const labelMids = labels.map((label) => label.mid);
  context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
  context.font = font;
  context.textAlign = "center";
  context.textBaseline = "middle";

  for (const label of labels) {
    const textWidth = Math.max(...label.lines.map((line) => context.measureText(line).width));
    const textHeight = lineHeight * label.lines.length;
    const baseFactor = label.percent < 10 ? 0.62 : 0.58;
    const preferredFactor = getInsidePieLabelRadiusFactor(
      label.mid,
      label.angle,
      labelMids,
      true,
      baseFactor,
    );
    const fallbackFactors = label.percent < 10
      ? [0.62, 0.8, 0.44, 0.9, 0.32]
      : [0.58, 0.72, 0.46];
    const radiusFactors = [preferredFactor, ...fallbackFactors.filter((factor) => factor !== preferredFactor)];
    let best = null;

    for (const factor of radiusFactors) {
      const x = centerX + Math.cos(label.mid) * radius * factor;
      const y = centerY + Math.sin(label.mid) * radius * factor;
      const rect = {
        left: x - textWidth / 2 - 4,
        right: x + textWidth / 2 + 4,
        top: y - textHeight / 2 - 4,
        bottom: y + textHeight / 2 + 4,
      };
      const overlap = placed.reduce((sum, other) => {
        const width = Math.max(0, Math.min(rect.right, other.right) - Math.max(rect.left, other.left));
        const height = Math.max(0, Math.min(rect.bottom, other.bottom) - Math.max(rect.top, other.top));
        return sum + width * height;
      }, 0);
      if (!best || overlap < best.overlap) best = { x, y, rect, overlap };
      if (overlap === 0) break;
    }

    placed.push(best.rect);
    drawPieLabelLines(context, label.lines, best.x, best.y, lineHeight);
  }
}

function getInsidePieLabelRadiusFactor(mid, angle, labelMids, compactPie, baseFactor) {
  const fullTurn = Math.PI * 2;
  const safeAngle = Math.min(fullTurn, Math.max(0, Math.abs(angle || 0)));
  const sectorCenterFactor = safeAngle > 0.0001
    ? (4 * Math.sin(safeAngle / 2)) / (3 * safeAngle)
    : 2 / 3;
  const visualCenterFactor = Math.min(
    compactPie ? 0.68 : 0.66,
    Math.max(baseFactor, sectorCenterFactor),
  );
  const nearestGap = labelMids.reduce((nearest, otherMid) => {
    if (otherMid === mid) return nearest;
    const rawGap = Math.abs(mid - otherMid) % fullTurn;
    return Math.min(nearest, Math.min(rawGap, fullTurn - rawGap));
  }, Infinity);
  const isolatedGap = compactPie ? Math.PI / 5.5 : Math.PI / 6;
  if (nearestGap < isolatedGap) return visualCenterFactor;
  return Math.min(compactPie ? 0.72 : 0.7, visualCenterFactor + 0.04);
}

function positionPieLabels(sideLabels, top, bottom, minGap) {
  const sorted = [...sideLabels].sort((a, b) => a.anchorY - b.anchorY);
  if (!sorted.length) return sorted;

  const gap = sorted.length > 1
    ? Math.min(minGap, (bottom - top) / (sorted.length - 1))
    : 0;
  const desired = sorted.map((label) =>
    Math.min(bottom, Math.max(top, label.targetY ?? label.anchorY))
  );
  const blocks = [];

  desired.forEach((value, index) => {
    blocks.push({ start: index, end: index, sum: value - index * gap, count: 1 });
    while (blocks.length > 1) {
      const current = blocks.at(-1);
      const previous = blocks.at(-2);
      if (previous.sum / previous.count <= current.sum / current.count) break;
      blocks.splice(-2, 2, {
        start: previous.start,
        end: current.end,
        sum: previous.sum + current.sum,
        count: previous.count + current.count,
      });
    }
  });

  const positions = new Array(sorted.length);
  const minLevel = top;
  const maxLevel = bottom - gap * (sorted.length - 1);
  blocks.forEach((block) => {
    const level = Math.min(maxLevel, Math.max(minLevel, block.sum / block.count));
    for (let index = block.start; index <= block.end; index += 1) {
      positions[index] = level + index * gap;
    }
  });

  sorted.forEach((label, index) => {
    label.markerY = positions[index];
  });

  return sorted;
}

function drawMobileOutsidePieLabels(context, labels, centerX, centerY, radius, height, width) {
  const top = 34;
  const bottom = height - 44;
  const lineHeight = 10;
  const markerSize = 7;

  for (const side of ["left", "right"]) {
    const sideLabels = positionPieLabels(
      labels.filter((label) => label.side === side),
      top - lineHeight,
      bottom - lineHeight,
      56
    );
    if (!sideLabels.length) continue;

    context.font = "700 8.5px sans-serif";
    const maxLineWidth = Math.max(...sideLabels.flatMap((label) => label.lines.map((line) => context.measureText(line).width)));
    const labelInset = Math.min(width / 2 - 18, Math.max(74, maxLineWidth + 8));

    for (const label of sideLabels) {
      const direction = side === "right" ? 1 : -1;
      const textX = side === "right" ? width - labelInset : labelInset;
      const markerX = textX - direction * 9;
      const markerY = label.markerY;
      label.y = markerY + lineHeight;
      drawPieLeaderLine(context, label, centerX, centerY, markerX, markerY, true);

      context.fillStyle = label.color;
      context.fillRect(markerX - markerSize / 2, markerY - markerSize / 2, markerSize, markerSize);
      context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.25)";
      context.strokeRect(markerX - markerSize / 2, markerY - markerSize / 2, markerSize, markerSize);

      context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
      context.textAlign = side === "right" ? "left" : "right";
      context.textBaseline = "middle";
      drawPieLabelLines(context, label.lines, textX, label.y, lineHeight);
    }
  }
}

function drawOutsidePieLabels(context, labels, centerX, radius, height) {
  const top = 24;
  const bottom = height - 38;
  const centerY = height / 2;

  for (const side of ["left", "right"]) {
    const sideLabels = positionPieLabels(
      labels.filter((label) => label.side === side),
      top,
      bottom,
      72
    );

    for (const label of sideLabels) {
      const direction = side === "right" ? 1 : -1;
      const textX = centerX + direction * (radius + 66);
      const markerSize = 8;
      const markerX = textX - direction * 12;
      const markerY = Math.abs(label.markerY - label.anchorY) < 1 ? label.anchorY : label.markerY;
      label.y = markerY + 14;
      drawPieLeaderLine(context, label, centerX, centerY, markerX, markerY, false);

      context.fillStyle = label.color;
      context.fillRect(markerX - markerSize / 2, markerY - markerSize / 2, markerSize, markerSize);
      context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.25)";
      context.strokeRect(markerX - markerSize / 2, markerY - markerSize / 2, markerSize, markerSize);

      context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
      context.font = "700 11.5px sans-serif";
      context.textAlign = side === "right" ? "left" : "right";
      context.textBaseline = "middle";
      drawPieLabelLines(context, label.lines, textX, label.y, 14);
    }
  }
}

function drawPieGroupSummary(context, rows, width, chartHeight, summaryHeight, compactPie, title = "中文股名小計", flowItems = false) {
  if (!rows.length) return;

  const x = 18;
  const y = chartHeight + 8;
  const panelWidth = width - 36;
  const panelHeight = summaryHeight - 16;

  context.fillStyle = "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.88)";
  context.strokeStyle = "rgba(220, 226, 220, 0.95)";
  context.lineWidth = 1;
  context.fillRect(x, y, panelWidth, panelHeight);
  context.strokeRect(x, y, panelWidth, panelHeight);

  if (compactPie) {
    drawMobilePieGroupSummary(context, rows, x, y, panelWidth, title, flowItems);
    return;
  }

  context.textAlign = "left";
  context.textBaseline = "middle";
  const padding = 12;
  let cursorX = x + padding;
  let rowY = y + 18;
  context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
  context.font = "700 13px sans-serif";
  context.fillText(title, cursorX, rowY);
  const titleWidth = context.measureText(title).width;
  const summaryTotal = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0);
  context.fillStyle = "#8a928d";
  context.font = "500 10.5px sans-serif";
  context.fillText(`總和：${money(summaryTotal)}`, cursorX + titleWidth + 8, rowY);
  const itemStartX = x + padding;
  const columnCount = 7;
  const columnWidth = (panelWidth - padding * 2) / columnCount;
  context.font = "700 11.5px sans-serif";
  const labels = rows.map((row) => `${row.group}: ${formatNumber(row.percent)}% ${money(row.value)}`);

  if (flowItems) {
    const maxX = x + panelWidth - padding;
    rowY = y + 44;
    rows.forEach((row, index) => {
      const labelWidth = context.measureText(labels[index]).width;
      const itemWidth = 12 + labelWidth;
      if (cursorX > itemStartX && cursorX + itemWidth > maxX) {
        cursorX = itemStartX;
        rowY += 24;
      }

      context.fillStyle = row.color;
      context.fillRect(cursorX, rowY - 4, 8, 8);
      context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.25)";
      context.strokeRect(cursorX, rowY - 4, 8, 8);

      context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
      context.fillText(labels[index], cursorX + 12, rowY);
      cursorX += itemWidth + 18;
    });
    return;
  }

  rows.forEach((row, index) => {
    const column = index % columnCount;
    const line = Math.floor(index / columnCount);
    cursorX = itemStartX + column * columnWidth;
    rowY = y + 44 + line * 24;
    const label = trimCanvasText(context, labels[index], columnWidth - 16);

    context.fillStyle = row.color;
    context.fillRect(cursorX, rowY - 4, 8, 8);
    context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.25)";
    context.strokeRect(cursorX, rowY - 4, 8, 8);

    context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
    context.fillText(label, cursorX + 12, rowY);
  });
}

function drawMobilePieGroupSummary(context, rows, x, y, width, title = "中文股名小計", showFullLabels = false) {
  const padding = 11;
  const rowGap = 20;
  let rowY = y + 18;

  context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
  context.font = "700 11.5px sans-serif";
  context.textAlign = "left";
  context.textBaseline = "middle";
  context.fillText(title, x + padding, rowY);
  const titleWidth = context.measureText(title).width;
  const summaryTotal = rows.reduce((sum, row) => sum + row.value, 0);
  context.fillStyle = "#8a928d";
  context.font = "500 9.5px sans-serif";
  context.fillText(`總和：${money(summaryTotal)}`, x + padding + titleWidth + 7, rowY);

  context.font = "700 10.5px sans-serif";
  rowY += 20;

  for (const row of rows) {
    const label = `${row.group}: ${formatNumber(row.percent)}% ${money(row.value)}`;
    const maxLabelWidth = width - padding * 2 - 14;
    let fontSize = 10.5;
    context.font = `700 ${fontSize}px sans-serif`;
    if (showFullLabels) {
      while (context.measureText(label).width > maxLabelWidth && fontSize > 7.5) {
        fontSize -= 0.5;
        context.font = `700 ${fontSize}px sans-serif`;
      }
    }
    context.fillStyle = row.color;
    context.fillRect(x + padding, rowY - 4, 8, 8);
    context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.25)";
    context.strokeRect(x + padding, rowY - 4, 8, 8);

    context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
    context.fillText(showFullLabels ? label : trimCanvasText(context, label, maxLabelWidth), x + padding + 13, rowY);
    rowY += rowGap;
  }
}

function getPieGroupSummaryHeight(rows, width, compactPie) {
  if (compactPie) return Math.max(174, 48 + rows.length * 20);
  const lineCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(rows.length / 7));
  return Math.max(72, 48 + lineCount * 24);
}

function getPieFlowSummaryHeight(context, rows, width, compactPie) {
  if (compactPie) return getPieGroupSummaryHeight(rows, width, compactPie);
  context.font = "700 11.5px sans-serif";
  const availableWidth = width - 60;
  let cursorWidth = 0;
  let lineCount = 1;
  for (const row of rows) {
    const label = `${row.group}: ${formatNumber(row.percent)}% ${money(row.value)}`;
    const itemWidth = 12 + context.measureText(label).width + 18;
    if (cursorWidth > 0 && cursorWidth + itemWidth > availableWidth) {
      lineCount += 1;
      cursorWidth = 0;
    }
    cursorWidth += itemWidth;
  }
  return Math.max(72, 48 + lineCount * 24);
}

function drawPieLabelLines(context, lines, x, y, lineHeight) {
  const offset = ((lines.length - 1) * lineHeight) / 2;
  lines.forEach((line, index) => context.fillText(line, x, y - offset + index * lineHeight));
}

function drawCompactPieLegend(context, slices, total, x, y, width, columns = 1) {
  context.textAlign = "left";
  context.textBaseline = "middle";
  context.font = "700 10px sans-serif";
  const gap = columns > 1 ? 24 : 0;
  const columnWidth = (width - gap * (columns - 1)) / columns;

  slices.forEach((slice, index) => {
    const column = index % columns;
    const row = Math.floor(index / columns);
    const rowX = x + column * (columnWidth + gap);
    const rowY = y + row * 24;
    const percent = (slice.value / total) * 100;
    const valueText = money(slice.value);
    const label = `${slice.label} ${formatNumber(percent)}% ${valueText}`;

    context.fillStyle = slice.color;
    context.fillRect(rowX, rowY - 6, 10, 10);
    context.strokeStyle = "rgba(23, 33, 28, 0.35)";
    context.lineWidth = 0.8;
    context.strokeRect(rowX, rowY - 6, 10, 10);

    context.fillStyle = "#17211c";
    context.fillText(trimCanvasText(context, label, columnWidth - 18), rowX + 16, rowY);
  });
}

function trimCanvasText(context, text, maxWidth) {
  if (context.measureText(text).width <= maxWidth) return text;
  let clipped = text;
  while (clipped.length > 3 && context.measureText(`${clipped}...`).width > maxWidth) {
    clipped = clipped.slice(0, -1);
  }
  return `${clipped}...`;
}

function drawGrid(context, padding, chartWidth, chartHeight, max, step = 500000, showRightAxis = false) {
  context.lineWidth = 1;
  for (let value = 0; value <= max; value += step) {
    const y = padding.top + chartHeight - (value / max) * chartHeight;
    context.strokeStyle = value === 0 ? "#17211c" : "rgba(108, 117, 111, 0.28)";
    context.beginPath();
    context.moveTo(padding.left, y);
    context.lineTo(padding.left + chartWidth, y);
    context.stroke();
  }

  context.strokeStyle = "#17211c";
  context.beginPath();
  context.moveTo(padding.left, padding.top);
  context.lineTo(padding.left, padding.top + chartHeight);
  context.lineTo(padding.left + chartWidth, padding.top + chartHeight);
  if (showRightAxis) {
    context.moveTo(padding.left + chartWidth, padding.top);
    context.lineTo(padding.left + chartWidth, padding.top + chartHeight);
  }
  context.stroke();

  context.fillStyle = "#6c756f";
  context.font = "12px sans-serif";
  context.textAlign = "right";
  context.textBaseline = "middle";
  for (let value = 0; value <= max; value += step) {
    const y = padding.top + chartHeight - (value / max) * chartHeight;
    const labelY = value === max ? y + 6 : y;
    context.fillText(formatMillionTick(value, step), padding.left - 12, labelY);
  }

  if (showRightAxis) {
    context.textAlign = "left";
    for (let value = 0; value <= max; value += step) {
      const y = padding.top + chartHeight - (value / max) * chartHeight;
      const labelY = value === max ? y + 6 : y;
      context.fillText(formatMillionTick(value, step), padding.left + chartWidth + 12, labelY);
    }
  }
}

function formatMillionTick(value, step = 500000) {
  if (!value) return "0";
  return `${formatNumber(value / 1000000, step < 100000 ? 2 : 1)}M`;
}

function calculateCryptoPosition(contract) {
  const marginUsdt = Math.max(0, toNumber(contract.marginUsdt));
  const leverage = Math.max(1, toNumber(contract.leverage));
  const quantity = Math.max(0, toNumber(contract.quantity));
  const entryPrice = Math.max(0, toNumber(contract.entryPrice));
  const currentPrice = Math.max(0, toNumber(contract.currentPrice));
  const fxRate = Math.max(0, toNumber(state.usdTwdRate));
  const notionalUsdt = quantity * entryPrice;
  const direction = contract.side === "short" ? -1 : 1;
  const rawPnlUsdt = quantity * (currentPrice - entryPrice) * direction;
  const equityUsdt = Math.max(0, marginUsdt + rawPnlUsdt);
  const pnlUsdt = equityUsdt - marginUsdt;
  const costTwd = marginUsdt * fxRate;
  const currentValueTwd = equityUsdt * fxRate;
  const pnlTwd = currentValueTwd - costTwd;
  const pnlPct = costTwd ? (pnlTwd / costTwd) * 100 : 0;
  return { marginUsdt, leverage, entryPrice, currentPrice, fxRate, notionalUsdt, quantity, pnlUsdt, equityUsdt, costTwd, currentValueTwd, pnlTwd, pnlPct };
}

function getPortfolioTotals() {
  const stockCost = state.holdings.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.shares * item.avgCost, 0);
  const stockTotal = state.holdings.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + item.shares * (item.currentPrice ?? item.avgCost),
    0,
  );
  const usStockCost = state.usHoldings.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + item.shares * item.avgCost * (state.usdTwdRate || 0),
    0,
  );
  const usStockMarketValue = state.usHoldings.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + item.shares * (item.currentPrice ?? item.avgCost) * (state.usdTwdRate || 0),
    0,
  );
  const usCashUsd = toNumber(state.usCashUsd);
  const usCashTwd = usCashUsd * (state.usdTwdRate || 0);
  const usStockTotal = usStockMarketValue + usCashTwd;
  const fundTotal = state.funds.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.currentValue || 0), 0);
  const fundCost = state.funds.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.cost || 0), 0);
  const cryptoPositions = state.cryptoContracts.map(calculateCryptoPosition);
  const cryptoTotal = cryptoPositions.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.currentValueTwd, 0);
  const cryptoCost = cryptoPositions.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.costTwd, 0);
  const cash = toNumber(state.cash);
  const cost = stockCost + usStockCost + fundCost + cryptoCost;
  const value = stockTotal + usStockTotal + fundTotal + cryptoTotal + cash;
  const stockPnl = stockTotal - stockCost;
  const usStockPnl = usStockMarketValue - usStockCost;
  const fundPnl = fundTotal - fundCost;
  const cryptoPnl = cryptoTotal - cryptoCost;
  const pnl = stockPnl + usStockPnl + fundPnl + cryptoPnl;
  const stockPnlPct = stockCost ? (stockPnl / stockCost) * 100 : 0;
  const usStockPnlPct = usStockCost ? (usStockPnl / usStockCost) * 100 : 0;
  const fundPnlPct = fundCost ? (fundPnl / fundCost) * 100 : 0;
  const cryptoPnlPct = cryptoCost ? (cryptoPnl / cryptoCost) * 100 : 0;
  const pnlPct = cost ? (pnl / cost) * 100 : 0;
  return { cost, value, cash, stockTotal, usStockTotal, usStockMarketValue, usCashUsd, usCashTwd, fundTotal, cryptoTotal, stockCost, usStockCost, fundCost, cryptoCost, stockPnl, usStockPnl, fundPnl, cryptoPnl, stockPnlPct, usStockPnlPct, fundPnlPct, cryptoPnlPct, pnl, pnlPct };
}

function backfillHistoryCosts() {
  const totals = getPortfolioTotals();
  let changed = false;

  for (const item of state.history) {
    if (!item.stockCost && item.stockTotal) {
      item.stockCost = totals.stockCost;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!item.fundCost && item.fundTotal) {
      item.fundCost = totals.fundCost;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(item.usStockTotal))) {
      item.usStockTotal = 0;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(item.usStockCost))) {
      item.usStockCost = 0;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(item.usCashUsd))) {
      item.usCashUsd = 0;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(item.usCashTwd))) {
      item.usCashTwd = 0;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(item.usStockMarketValue))) {
      item.usStockMarketValue = toNumber(item.usStockTotal) - toNumber(item.usCashTwd);
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(item.cash))) {
      item.cash = 0;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (!hasRecordedCash(item) && toNumber(item.cash) !== 0) {
      item.cash = 0;
      item.cashRecorded = false;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (hasRecordedCash(item) && item.cashRecorded !== true) {
      item.cashRecorded = true;
      changed = true;
    }
    const nextCost = toNumber(item.stockCost) + toNumber(item.usStockCost) + toNumber(item.fundCost) + toNumber(item.cryptoCost);
    const nextTotal = toNumber(item.stockTotal) + toNumber(item.usStockTotal) + toNumber(item.fundTotal) + toNumber(item.cryptoTotal) + getRecordedCash(item);
    if (nextTotal && item.total !== nextTotal) {
      item.total = nextTotal;
      changed = true;
    }
    if (nextCost && item.cost !== nextCost) {
      item.cost = nextCost;
      changed = true;
    }
    const nextPnl = toNumber(item.stockTotal) + toNumber(item.usStockMarketValue) + toNumber(item.fundTotal) + toNumber(item.cryptoTotal) - nextCost;
    if (nextCost && item.pnl !== nextPnl) {
      item.pnl = nextPnl;
      changed = true;
    }
  }

  if (changed) persistLocalState();
}

function saveAndRender(message = "", syncImmediately = false) {
  persistLocalState();
  render();
  if (syncImmediately && cloudClient) {
    window.clearTimeout(cloudSaveTimer);
    if (message) setStatus(`${message} 正在同步雲端...`);
    void syncStateToCloud();
    return;
  }
  queueCloudSave();
  if (message) setStatus(message);
}

function setStatus(message) {
  els.statusText.textContent = message;
}

function loadState() {
  const fallback = { holdings: [], usHoldings: [], funds: [], cryptoContracts: [], history: [], tradeHistory: [], currentFundTotal: 0, cash: 0, usdTwdRate: 0, usCashUsd: 0 };
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    return raw ? migrateState(JSON.parse(raw)) : fallback;
  } catch {
    return fallback;
  }
}

function persistLocalState() {
  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
}

async function initCloudSync() {
  if (!CLOUD_CONFIG.url || !CLOUD_CONFIG.anonKey) {
    setStatus("目前是本機暫存模式；設定 Supabase 後會自動同步雲端。");
    return;
  }

  try {
    setStatus("正在連接雲端資料...");
    await loadSupabaseClient();
    cloudClient = window.supabase.createClient(CLOUD_CONFIG.url, CLOUD_CONFIG.anonKey);

    const { data, error } = await cloudClient
      .from(CLOUD_TABLE)
      .select("data")
      .eq("id", CLOUD_ROW_ID)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) throw error;

    if (data?.data) {
      replaceState(migrateState(data.data));
      persistLocalState();
      render();
      setStatus("已載入雲端資料。");
      return;
    }

    await syncStateToCloud();
    setStatus("已建立第一份雲端資料。");
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    setStatus("雲端連線失敗，目前先保留本機資料。請檢查 Supabase 設定。");
  }
}

function replaceState(nextState) {
  state.holdings = nextState.holdings;
  state.usHoldings = nextState.usHoldings;
  state.funds = nextState.funds;
  state.cryptoContracts = nextState.cryptoContracts;
  state.history = nextState.history;
  state.tradeHistory = nextState.tradeHistory;
  state.currentFundTotal = nextState.currentFundTotal;
  state.cash = nextState.cash;
  state.usdTwdRate = nextState.usdTwdRate;
  state.usCashUsd = nextState.usCashUsd;
  els.cash.value = state.cash ? String(state.cash) : "";
  els.usdTwdRate.value = state.usdTwdRate ? String(state.usdTwdRate) : "";
  els.usCashUsd.value = state.usCashUsd ? String(state.usCashUsd) : "";
}

function loadSupabaseClient() {
  if (window.supabase?.createClient) return Promise.resolve();

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const script = document.createElement("script");
    script.src = "https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@supabase/supabase-js@2";
    script.onload = () => resolve();
    script.onerror = () => reject(new Error("Supabase library failed to load"));
    document.head.append(script);
  });
}

function queueCloudSave() {
  if (!cloudClient) return;
  window.clearTimeout(cloudSaveTimer);
  cloudSaveTimer = window.setTimeout(syncStateToCloud, 600);
}

async function syncStateToCloud() {
  if (!cloudClient) return;

  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state));
    const { error } = await cloudClient.from(CLOUD_TABLE).upsert(
      {
        id: CLOUD_ROW_ID,
        data: payload,
        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      { onConflict: "id" },
    );
    if (error) throw error;
    setStatus("已同步到雲端。");
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(error);
    setStatus("雲端同步失敗，目前先存在本機。");
  }
}

function migrateState(value) {
  if (!value || !Array.isArray(value.holdings) || !Array.isArray(value.history)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid data");
  }

  const legacyUsRate = Array.isArray(value.usHoldings)
    ? value.usHoldings.find((holding) => toNumber(holding.fxRate) > 0)?.fxRate
    : 0;
  const legacyCryptoRate = Array.isArray(value.cryptoContracts)
    ? value.cryptoContracts.find((contract) => toNumber(contract.fxRate) > 0)?.fxRate
    : 0;
  const usdTwdRate = toNumber(value.usdTwdRate) || toNumber(legacyUsRate) || toNumber(legacyCryptoRate);

  const holdings = value.holdings.map((holding) => ({
    id: holding.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
    symbol: normalizeSymbol(holding.symbol || ""),
    name: String(holding.name || ""),
    shares: toNumber(holding.shares),
    avgCost: toNumber(holding.avgCost),
    currentPrice: toOptionalNumber(holding.currentPrice),
    purchaseDate: parseDateInput(holding.purchaseDate) || LEGACY_PURCHASE_DATE,
    lastUpdated: String(holding.lastUpdated || ""),
  }));

  const usHoldings = Array.isArray(value.usHoldings)
    ? value.usHoldings.map((holding) => ({
        id: holding.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
        symbol: normalizeSymbol(holding.symbol || ""),
        shares: toNumber(holding.shares),
        avgCost: toNumber(holding.avgCost),
        currentPrice: toOptionalNumber(holding.currentPrice),
        purchaseDate: parseDateInput(holding.purchaseDate) || LEGACY_PURCHASE_DATE,
        lastUpdated: String(holding.lastUpdated || ""),
      }))
    : [];

  let funds = Array.isArray(value.funds)
    ? value.funds.map((fund) => ({
        id: fund.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
        name: String(fund.name || "未命名基金"),
        cost: Number.isFinite(Number(fund.cost)) ? toNumber(fund.cost) : toNumber(fund.currentValue),
        currentValue: toNumber(fund.currentValue),
        lastUpdated: String(fund.lastUpdated || ""),
      }))
    : [];

  const cryptoContracts = Array.isArray(value.cryptoContracts)
    ? value.cryptoContracts.map((contract) => {
        const leverage = Math.max(1, toNumber(contract.leverage));
        if (Number.isFinite(Number(contract.marginUsdt))) {
          const entryPrice = toNumber(contract.entryPrice);
          const marginUsdt = toNumber(contract.marginUsdt);
          return {
            id: contract.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
            name: normalizeSymbol(contract.name || "BTCUSDT"),
            side: contract.side === "short" ? "short" : "long",
            leverage,
            marginUsdt,
            quantity: Number.isFinite(Number(contract.quantity))
              ? toNumber(contract.quantity)
              : (entryPrice ? (marginUsdt * leverage) / entryPrice : 0),
            entryPrice,
            currentPrice: toNumber(contract.currentPrice),
          };
        }

        const legacyCost = toNumber(contract.cost);
        const legacyValue = toNumber(contract.currentValue);
        const legacyMove = legacyCost ? (legacyValue - legacyCost) / (legacyCost * leverage) : 0;
        return {
          id: contract.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
          name: normalizeSymbol(contract.name || "BTCUSDT"),
          side: "long",
          leverage,
          marginUsdt: legacyCost,
          quantity: legacyCost * leverage,
          entryPrice: 1,
          currentPrice: Math.max(0, 1 + legacyMove),
        };
      })
    : [];

  const latestHistoryFundTotal = [...value.history]
    .sort((a, b) => String(a.date || "").localeCompare(String(b.date || "")))
    .at(-1)?.fundTotal;
  const legacyFundTotal = toNumber(value.currentFundTotal) || toNumber(latestHistoryFundTotal);
  if (!funds.length && legacyFundTotal > 0) {
    funds = [
      {
        id: "legacy-fund-total",
        name: "基金總額",
        cost: legacyFundTotal,
        currentValue: legacyFundTotal,
        lastUpdated: "舊資料",
      },
    ];
  }

  const history = value.history
    .map((item) => {
      const fundTotal = toNumber(item.fundTotal);
      const cryptoTotal = toNumber(item.cryptoTotal);
      const stockTotal = Number.isFinite(Number(item.stockTotal))
        ? toNumber(item.stockTotal)
        : Math.max(toNumber(item.total) - fundTotal, 0);
      const stockCost = Number.isFinite(Number(item.stockCost)) ? toNumber(item.stockCost) : 0;
      const usStockTotal = Number.isFinite(Number(item.usStockTotal)) ? toNumber(item.usStockTotal) : 0;
      const usCashUsd = Number.isFinite(Number(item.usCashUsd)) ? toNumber(item.usCashUsd) : 0;
      const usCashTwd = Number.isFinite(Number(item.usCashTwd)) ? toNumber(item.usCashTwd) : 0;
      const usStockMarketValue = Number.isFinite(Number(item.usStockMarketValue))
        ? toNumber(item.usStockMarketValue)
        : usStockTotal - usCashTwd;
      const usStockCost = Number.isFinite(Number(item.usStockCost)) ? toNumber(item.usStockCost) : 0;
      const fundCost = Number.isFinite(Number(item.fundCost)) ? toNumber(item.fundCost) : 0;
      const cryptoCost = Number.isFinite(Number(item.cryptoCost)) ? toNumber(item.cryptoCost) : 0;
      const rawCash = Number.isFinite(Number(item.cash)) ? toNumber(item.cash) : 0;
      const cashRecorded = Boolean(item.cashRecorded) || (String(item.date || "") >= "2026-07-15" && rawCash > 0);
      const cash = cashRecorded ? rawCash : 0;
      const cost = stockCost + usStockCost + fundCost + cryptoCost;
      return {
        date: String(item.date || todayISO()),
        total: stockTotal + usStockTotal + fundTotal + cryptoTotal + cash,
        stockTotal,
        usStockTotal,
        usStockMarketValue,
        usCashUsd,
        usCashTwd,
        fundTotal,
        cryptoTotal,
        cash,
        cashRecorded,
        stockCost,
        usStockCost,
        fundCost,
        cryptoCost,
        cost,
        pnl: stockTotal + usStockMarketValue + fundTotal + cryptoTotal - cost,
        prices: item.prices && typeof item.prices === "object" ? item.prices : {},
        usPrices: item.usPrices && typeof item.usPrices === "object" ? item.usPrices : {},
        fundValues: item.fundValues && typeof item.fundValues === "object" ? item.fundValues : {},
        cryptoValues: item.cryptoValues && typeof item.cryptoValues === "object" ? item.cryptoValues : {},
        note: String(item.note || ""),
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));

  const tradeHistory = Array.isArray(value.tradeHistory)
    ? value.tradeHistory.map((trade) => {
        const market = trade.market === "us" ? "us" : "tw";
        const shares = Math.max(0, toNumber(trade.shares));
        const avgCost = Number.isFinite(Number(trade.avgCost))
          ? Math.max(0, toNumber(trade.avgCost))
          : (shares ? Math.max(0, toNumber(trade.costNative)) / shares : 0);
        const sellPrice = Number.isFinite(Number(trade.sellPrice))
          ? Math.max(0, toNumber(trade.sellPrice))
          : (shares ? Math.max(0, toNumber(trade.proceedsNative)) / shares : 0);
        return createTradeRecord({
          id: trade.id || crypto.randomUUID(),
          market,
          symbol: trade.symbol,
          name: trade.name,
          buyDate: trade.buyDate,
          sellDate: trade.sellDate,
          shares,
          avgCost,
          sellPrice,
          fxRate: market === "us" ? toNumber(trade.fxRate) : 1,
          recordedAt: trade.recordedAt,
        });
      })
    : [];

  return {
    holdings,
    usHoldings,
    funds,
    cryptoContracts,
    history,
    tradeHistory,
    currentFundTotal: funds.reduce((sum, fund) => sum + fund.currentValue, 0),
    cash: toNumber(value.cash),
    usdTwdRate,
    usCashUsd: toNumber(value.usCashUsd),
  };
}

function findHolding(id) {
  return state.holdings.find((item) => item.id === id);
}

function findUsHolding(id) {
  return state.usHoldings.find((item) => item.id === id);
}

function findFund(id) {
  return state.funds.find((item) => item.id === id);
}

function findCryptoContract(id) {
  return state.cryptoContracts.find((item) => item.id === id);
}

    </svg>
  `;
}

function normalizeSymbol(value) {
  return value.trim().toUpperCase();
}

function getCryptoBaseAsset(value) {
  const symbol = normalizeSymbol(String(value || ""));
  const quoteAssets = ["USDT", "USDC", "BUSD", "USD", "TWD", "BTC", "ETH"];
  const quoteAsset = quoteAssets.find((quote) => symbol.endsWith(quote) && symbol.length > quote.length);
  return quoteAsset ? symbol.slice(0, -quoteAsset.length) : (symbol || "資產");
}

function toNumber(value) {
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : 0;
}

function toOptionalNumber(value) {
  if (value === "" || value === null || value === undefined) return null;
  const number = Number(value);
  return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : null;
}

function money(value) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("zh-TW", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "TWD",
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(value || 0);
}

function usdMoney(value) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-US", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "USD",
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(value || 0);
}

function unitMoney(value) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("zh-TW", {
    style: "currency",
    currency: "TWD",
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(value || 0);
}

function signedMoney(value) {
  return `${value >= 0 ? "+" : "-"}${money(Math.abs(value))}`;
}

function signedUsdMoney(value) {
  return `${value >= 0 ? "+" : "-"}${usdMoney(Math.abs(value))}`;
}

function signedNumber(value, digits = 2) {
  return `${value >= 0 ? "+" : "-"}${formatNumber(Math.abs(value), digits)}`;
}

function signedPercent(value) {
  return `${value >= 0 ? "+" : "-"}${formatNumber(Math.abs(value))}%`;
}

function shortMoney(value) {
  if (Math.abs(value) >= 100000000) return `${formatNumber(value / 100000000, 0)}億`;
  if (Math.abs(value) >= 10000) return `${formatNumber(value / 10000, 0)}萬`;
  return formatNumber(value, 0);
}

function formatNumber(value, digits = 0) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("zh-TW", {
    maximumFractionDigits: digits,
    minimumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(value || 0);
}

function todayISO() {
  const date = new Date();
  const year = date.getFullYear();
  const month = String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const day = String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
}

function formatDateForInput(value) {
  const iso = parseDateInput(value);
  return iso ? iso.replaceAll("-", "/") : "";
}

function parseDateInput(value) {
  const text = String(value || "").trim();
  const match = text.match(/^(\d{4})[-/.](\d{1,2})[-/.](\d{1,2})$/);
  if (!match) return "";
  const year = Number(match[1]);
  const month = Number(match[2]);
  const day = Number(match[3]);
  const date = new Date(year, month - 1, day);
  if (date.getFullYear() !== year || date.getMonth() !== month - 1 || date.getDate() !== day) return "";
  return `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(day).padStart(2, "0")}`;
}

function escapeHTML(value) {
  return String(value)
    .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
    .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
    .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
    .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
    .replaceAll("'", "&#039;");
}

backfillHistoryCosts();
render();
initCloudSync();

===== requirements.txt =====
streamlit>=1.45

===== streamlit_app.py =====
import json
import hmac
import re
from pathlib import Path

import streamlit as st


ROOT = Path(__file__).parent


def read_text(filename: str) -> str:
    return (ROOT / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def build_page() -> str:
    html = read_text("index.html")
    css = read_text("styles.css")
    js = read_text("app.js")

    supabase = st.secrets.get("supabase", {})
    config = {
        "url": supabase.get("url", ""),
        "anonKey": supabase.get("anon_key", ""),
        "table": supabase.get("table", "stock_ledger_state"),
        "rowId": supabase.get("row_id", "main"),
    }

    html = re.sub(
        r'<link\s+rel="stylesheet"\s+href="styles\.css[^"]*"\s*/>',
        lambda _: f"<style>{css}</style>",
        html,
        count=1,
    )
    html = re.sub(
        r'<script\s+src="app\.js[^"]*"></script>',
        lambda _: (
            "<script>"
            f"window.STOCK_LEDGER_SUPABASE = {json.dumps(config, ensure_ascii=False)};"
            "</script>"
            f"<script>{js}</script>"
        ),
        html,
        count=1,
    )
    return html


def check_password() -> bool:
    app_config = st.secrets.get("app", {})
    expected_password = app_config.get("password", "")

    if not expected_password:
        st.warning("請先在 Streamlit Secrets 設定 app.password。")
        return False

    if st.session_state.get("password_ok"):
        return True

    password = st.text_input("Ledger, passwords please!", type="password")
    if not password:
        return False

    if hmac.compare_digest(password, expected_password):
        st.session_state["password_ok"] = True
        st.rerun()

    st.error("密碼不正確")
    return False


st.set_page_config(page_title="Stock Ledger!", page_icon="💰", layout="wide")

if check_password():
    st.components.v1.html(build_page(), height=1800, scrolling=True)

===== supabase_setup.sql =====
create table if not exists public.stock_ledger_state (
  id text primary key,
  data jsonb not null,
  updated_at timestamptz not null default now()
);

alter table public.stock_ledger_state enable row level security;

grant usage on schema public to anon;
grant select, insert, update on public.stock_ledger_state to anon;

drop policy if exists "stock ledger read" on public.stock_ledger_state;
drop policy if exists "stock ledger insert" on public.stock_ledger_state;
drop policy if exists "stock ledger update" on public.stock_ledger_state;

create policy "stock ledger read"
on public.stock_ledger_state
for select
to anon
using (id = 'main');

create policy "stock ledger insert"
on public.stock_ledger_state
for insert
to anon
with check (id = 'main');

create policy "stock ledger update"
on public.stock_ledger_state
for update
to anon
using (id = 'main')
with check (id = 'main');

===== releases/L1.5.2/README_L1.5.2.txt =====
L1.5.2

- 修正刪除台股或美股時的交易紀錄視窗：固定置中於目前可見畫面，不再自動聚焦日期欄位；手機可視高度縮小時，三個操作按鈕仍固定在視窗內可直接點選。
- 交易紀錄名稱改為「台股 / 中文名 / 股號」；美股改為「美股 / 名稱」。
- Excel 匯出改用瀏覽器 Blob 下載流程，不再依賴套件內建下載；按鈕會直接顯示產生中、下載完成或失敗狀態。
- Excel 檔案已用台股與美股測試資料產生並重新讀回，確認欄位與內容可正常解析。
- 既有持股、每日紀錄、折線圖、圓餅圖與其他排版不變。
